inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.NAME, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON i.VIF_TYPE = v.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column ORDER BY i.MAC_ADDRESS) SELECT dipw.port_id, dipw.tenant_id, dipw.host, dipw.vif_type, dipw.ip_address, dipw.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, dipw.total_down_ports, dipw.avg_down_port_count, dapwf.NAME AS port_name, dapwf.MAC_ADDRESS, dapwf.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dapwf.window_column AS admin_state_up_window_avg FROM down_ports_ip_window AS dipw JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = dipw.port_id JOIN down_ports_admin_window_filtered AS dapwf ON dapwf.MAC_ADDRESS = p.MAC_ADDRESS AND dapwf.HOST = dipw.host AND dapwf.VIF_TYPE = dipw.vif_type
WITH sg_admin_up_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), down_ports_tenant_benchmark AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON i.tenant_id = tdp.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp) SELECT sap.port_id, sap.tenant_id, sap.port_name, sap.security_group_binding_count, sap.dns_record_count, sap.deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count, dptb.mac_address, dptb.window_column, dptb.total_down_ports, dptb.benchmark, dptb.user_category FROM sg_admin_up_ports AS sap JOIN down_ports_tenant_benchmark AS dptb ON sap.port_id = dptb.port_id WHERE NOT sap.tenant_id IS NULL
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ia.SUBNET_ID AS subnet_id, AVG(p.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS), SubnetAllocations AS (SELECT ia.subnet_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.ip_address) AS allocated_ips FROM IPALLOCATIONS AS ia GROUP BY ia.subnet_id), AverageAllocations AS (SELECT AVG(allocated_ips) AS avg_ips FROM SubnetAllocations) SELECT i.port_id, i.port_name, i.mac_address, i.ip_address, i.subnet_id, i.window_column, sa.allocated_ips AS subnet_allocated_ips, (sa.allocated_ips - aa.avg_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SubnetAllocations AS sa ON i.subnet_id = sa.subnet_id CROSS JOIN AverageAllocations AS aa ORDER BY i.ip_address), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id AS ports_tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations, SUM(inner_cte.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, SUM(inner_cte.subnet_id_rows) AS total_subnet_id_rows, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_per_tenant FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id, avg_ports.avg_port_count), TenantDownPortSubnetCoverage AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.subnet_id) AS down_port_subnet_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT q2.ports_tenant_id AS tenant_id, q2.total_admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations, q2.total_ip_allocations, q2.total_subnet_id_rows, q2.avg_ports_per_tenant, tdc.down_port_subnet_count FROM q2 JOIN TenantDownPortSubnetCoverage AS tdc ON tdc.tenant_id = q2.ports_tenant_id WHERE tdc.down_port_subnet_count > (SELECT AVG(down_port_subnet_count) * 1.0 FROM TenantDownPortSubnetCoverage)
WITH down_ports_top10 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.port_id) AS total_down_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT t.tenant_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_tenant_with_multiple_down_ports FROM inner_cte LEFT JOIN (SELECT tenant_id FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) > 1) AS t ON inner_cte.tenant_id = t.tenant_id GROUP BY inner_cte.security_group_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC LIMIT 10), sg_geomean AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC) SELECT inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, avg_sg.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) AS avg_sg ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC) SELECT dpt.security_group_id, dpt.total_down_ports, dpt.has_tenant_with_multiple_down_ports, sg.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, sg.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM down_ports_top10 AS dpt LEFT JOIN sg_geomean AS sg ON dpt.security_group_id = sg.security_group_id ORDER BY dpt.total_down_ports DESC
WITH down_ports_sg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), sg_counts AS (SELECT security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, c.down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN sg_counts AS c ON i.security_group_id = c.security_group_id WHERE c.down_ports > (SELECT AVG(down_ports) FROM sg_counts)), host_drive59_tenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.PORT_ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT psb.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_port_security_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON i.PORT_ID = psb.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_ports DESC) SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.mac_address, d.security_group_id, d.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, d.down_ports AS down_ports_in_security_group, h.total_ports AS total_ports_on_drive_59_for_tenant, h.has_port_security_enabled FROM down_ports_sg AS d JOIN host_drive59_tenants AS h ON d.tenant_id = h.tenant_id
WITH down_secure_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1) SELECT p.tenant_id AS ports_tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled, avg_counts.avg_down_secure_port_count FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_down_secure_port_count FROM (SELECT p2.tenant_id, COUNT(p2.id) AS port_count FROM ports AS p2 JOIN AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up, SUM(inner_cte.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings, SUM(inner_cte.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up_per_tenant FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS ROUND(CAST(dsp.total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS REAL) / NULLIF(sap.total_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up, 0), 2) AS down_secure_to_sg_admin_up_port_ratio FROM sg_admin_up_ports AS sap WITH active_network_latest_stdattr AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT tenant_id, MAX(standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_standard_attr_updated_at FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), top10_default_external_tenants AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.NETWORK_ID) AS total_default_external_networks, MIN(i.MTU) AS min_mtu_in_tenant FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_default_external_networks DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT t.TENANT_ID, t.total_default_external_networks, t.min_mtu_in_tenant, a.latest_standard_attr_updated_at FROM top10_default_external_tenants AS t JOIN active_network_latest_stdattr AS a ON t.TENANT_ID = a.tenant_id ORDER BY t.total_default_external_networks DESC

WITH deviation_by_status_owner AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), above_avg_groups AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), GroupAverage AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM inner_cte AS i WHERE i.port_count > (SELECT avg_port_count FROM GroupAverage)) SELECT a.port_status, a.device_owner, a.port_count, a.earliest_standard_attr_update, d.deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM above_avg_groups AS a JOIN deviation_by_status_owner AS d ON d.port_status = a.port_status AND d.device_owner = a.device_owner AND d.port_count = a.port_count AND d.earliest_standard_attr_update = a.earliest_standard_attr_update
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AvgMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, a.avg_mtu AS benchmark_avg_mtu, CASE WHEN i.mtu > a.avg_mtu THEN 'ABOVE_AVG_MTU' WHEN i.mtu < a.avg_mtu THEN 'BELOW_AVG_MTU' ELSE 'AT_AVG_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMtu AS a), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.NAME, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON i.VIF_TYPE = v.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column ORDER BY i.MAC_ADDRESS), q2_ports AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, q2.NAME AS port_name, q2.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, q2.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS port_admin_state_up, q2.HOST AS binding_host, q2.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, q2.window_column AS port_admin_state_up_window_avg FROM q2 JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.MAC_ADDRESS = q2.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.NAME = q2.NAME WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT q1.network_id, q1.network_name, q1.tenant_id, q1.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, q1.mtu, q1.standard_attr_updated_at, q1.window_column AS tenant_network_name_rownum, q1.benchmark_avg_mtu, q1.mtu_category, q2_ports.port_name, q2_ports.mac_address, q2_ports.port_admin_state_up, q2_ports.binding_host, q2_ports.vif_type, q2_ports.port_admin_state_up_window_avg FROM q1 JOIN q2_ports ON q1.network_id = q2_ports.network_id ORDER BY q1.tenant_id, q1.network_name, q2_ports.mac_address

pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE'), ProviderAverages AS (SELECT pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' GROUP BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME) SELECT i.loadbalancer_id, i.loadbalancer_name, i.provider_name, i.vip_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = i.loadbalancer_id WHERE stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS > (SELECT pa.avg_total_connections FROM ProviderAverages AS pa WHERE pa.provider_name = i.provider_name)), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), haproxy_avg AS (SELECT AVG(stats2.BYTES_IN * 1.0) AS avg_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats2 JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 ON stats2.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb2.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT i.loadbalancer_name, i.vip_address, i.bytes_in, i.total_connections, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN haproxy_avg AS a WHERE i.bytes_in > a.avg_bytes_in ORDER BY i.loadbalancer_name) SELECT q1.loadbalancer_id, q1.loadbalancer_name, q1.provider_name, q1.vip_address, q1.window_column AS provider_rank_by_total_connections, q2.bytes_in, q2.total_connections, q2.window_column AS haproxy_row_number_window FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.loadbalancer_name = q2.loadbalancer_name AND q1.vip_address = q2.vip_address WHERE q1.provider_name = 'haproxy'

a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a), down_ports_by_sg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON i.tenant_id = tdp.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp) SELECT sap.port_id, sap.tenant_id, sap.port_name, sap.security_group_binding_count, sap.dns_record_count, sap.deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count, dps.mac_address, dps.window_column, dps.total_down_ports, dps.benchmark, dps.user_category FROM sg_admin_ports AS sap JOIN down_ports_by_sg AS dps ON sap.port_id = dps.port_id AND sap.tenant_id = dps.tenant_id

vts.avg_running_sum_in_vif_type) AS deviation_from_avg_running_sum_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = ic.PORT_ID JOIN VifTypeStats AS vts ON vts.VIF_TYPE = b.VIF_TYPE), tenant_down_secure_port_stats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.TENANT_ID, tdsp.down_secure_ports, adsp.avg_down_secure_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports > adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports < adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS overall_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageDownSecurePorts AS adsp CROSS JOIN inner_cte) SELECT pvrs.PORT_ID, pvrs.TENANT_ID, pvrs.MAC_ADDRESS, pvrs.running_sum_standard_attr_id, pvrs.ports_in_vif_type, pvrs.deviation_from_avg_running_sum_in_vif_type, tdsp.down_secure_ports, tdsp.benchmark, tdsp.user_category, tdsp.overall_down_secure_ports FROM port_vif_running_sums AS pvrs JOIN tenant_down_secure_port_stats AS tdsp ON tdsp.TENANT_ID = pvrs.TENANT_ID

r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SubnetRouteCounts AS (SELECT s.ID AS subnet_id, COUNT(r.DESTINATION) AS total_routes FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN SUBNETROUTES AS r ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.ID), AverageRoutes AS (SELECT AVG(total_routes) src.total_routes > ar.avg_routes * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_ROUTE_SUBNET' WHEN src.total_routes < ar.avg_routes * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_ROUTE_SUBNET' ELSE 'AVERAGE_ROUTE_SUBNET' END AS route_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr AND s.NAME = i.subnet_name JOIN SubnetRouteCounts AS src ON src.subnet_id = s.ID CROSS JOIN AverageRoutes AS ar), ActiveNetworkDetails AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AvgMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, a.avg_mtu AS benchmark_avg_mtu, CASE WHEN i.mtu > a.avg_mtu THEN 'ABOVE_AVG_MTU' WHEN i.mtu < a.avg_mtu THEN 'BELOW_AVG_MTU' ELSE 'AT_AVG_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMtu AS a) SELECT rd.DESTINATION, rd.NEXTHOP, RouteDetails AS rd JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.CIDR = rd.subnet_cidr AND s.NAME = rd.subnet_name JOIN ActiveNetworkDetails AS andt ON andt.network_id = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 ORDER BY andt.tenant_id, andt.network_name, rd.subnet_name, rd.DESTINATION

ahc.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN HostCounts AS hc ON i.host = hc.host CROSS JOIN AverageHostCounts AS ahc), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.TENANT_ID, tdsp.down_secure_ports, adsp.avg_down_secure_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports > adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports < adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS overall_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageDownSecurePorts AS adsp CROSS JOIN inner_cte) SELECT q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.host, q1.vif_type, q1.ip_address, q1.window_column, q1.down_allocated_ips, q1.deviation_from_avg, q2.down_secure_ports, q2.benchmark AS avg_down_secure_ports_benchmark, q2.user_category, q2.overall_down_secure_ports FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id ORDER BY q1.host, q1.ip_address
WITH down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), NetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id), AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM NetworkDownAllocations) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.device_owner, i.ip_address, i.window_column, nda.down_allocated_ips, (nda.down_allocated_ips - anda.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkDownAllocations AS nda ON i.network_id = nda.network_id CROSS JOIN AverageNetworkDownAllocations AS anda), active_networks AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AvgMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, a.avg_mtu AS benchmark_avg_mtu, CASE WHEN i.mtu > a.avg_mtu THEN 'ABOVE_AVG_MTU' WHEN i.mtu < a.avg_mtu THEN 'BELOW_AVG_MTU' ELSE 'AT_AVG_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgMtu AS a) SELECT dp.port_id, dp.tenant_id AS port_tenant_id, dp.port_name, dp.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id AS network_tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_rownum_within_tenant, an.benchmark_avg_mtu, an.mtu_category, dp.mac_address, dp.device_owner, dp.ip_address, dp.window_column AS port_rownum_within_network, dp.down_allocated_ips, dp.deviation_from_avg FROM down_ports AS dp JOIN active_networks AS an ON dp.network_id = an.network_id

aa.avg_ip_allocations AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.ip_allocation_count > aa.avg_ip_allocations * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_IP' WHEN ic.ip_allocation_count < aa.avg_ip_allocations * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_IP' ELSE tenant_id, port_id, signal_type

AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)) SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM q1) AS Total_Ports_Tenant_98333_Admin_Up, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM q2) AS Total_Ports_Drive_59_Tenant_17ea_Above_Avg_RunningSum, ROUND(CAST((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM q2) AS REAL) / NULLIF((SELECT COUNT(*) FROM q1), 0), 4) AS Ratio_Drive_59_AboveAvg_To_Tenant_98333_AdminUp

WITH AdminUpHighWindowedAvgPorts AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON v.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column), DownPortsHighStdAttrInSecurityGroups AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), TenantAvg AS ta.avg_standard_attr_id) SELECT a.PORT_ID, a.HOST, a.VIF_TYPE, a.VNIC_TYPE, a.TENANT_ID, a.NETWORK_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS, a.STATUS, a.DEVICE_OWNER, a.window_column AS admin_up_windowed_avg_standard_attr_id FROM AdminUpHighWindowedAvgPorts AS a WHERE a.PORT_ID IN (SELECT d.port_id FROM DownPortsHighStdAttrInSecurityGroups AS d) ORDER BY a.NETWORK_ID, a.PORT_ID
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.min_standard_attr_id, avg_counts.avg_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_status, ic.device_owner, ic.port_count, ic.min_standard_attr_id, ac.avg_port_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.port_count > ac.avg_port_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_GROUP' WHEN ic.port_count < ac.avg_port_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_GROUP' ELSE 'AVERAGE_GROUP' END AS group_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac) SELECT s1.port_status, s1.device_owner, s1.port_count AS distinct_port_count, s1.min_standard_attr_id, s1.avg_port_count AS overall_avg_distinct_port_count, s2.benchmark AS overall_avg_port_count_benchmark, s2.group_category FROM subq1 AS s1 JOIN subq2 AS s2 ON s1.port_status = s2.port_status AND s1.device_owner = s2.device_owner AND s1.port_count = s2.port_count AND s1.min_standard_attr_id = s2.min_standard_attr_id

a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_mtu_cte AS a), ports_sg_ranked AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupAverages AS (SELECT AVG(sg_port_count) AS avg_ports_per_sg FROM (SELECT sgpb2.security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS sg_port_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON sgpb2.port_id = p2.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb2 ON p2.id = psb2.port_id GROUP BY sgpb2.security_group_id) AS sg_counts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT sgpb3.security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS total_ports_in_sg FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb3 JOIN PORTS AS p3 ON sgpb3.port_id = p3.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb3 ON p3.id = psb3.port_id GROUP BY sgpb3.security_group_id) AS sg_totals ON sg_totals.security_group_id = i.security_group_id WHERE sg_totals.total_ports_in_sg > (SELECT avg_ports_per_sg FROM SecurityGroupAverages)) SELECT nmd.network_id, nmd.network_name, nmd.tenant_id, nmd.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, nmd.mtu, nmd.standard_attr_updated_at AS network_standard_attr_updated_at, nmd.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, nmd.deviation_from_avg_mtu, psr.port_id, psr.mac_address, psr.security_group_id, psr.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group FROM networks_mtu_detail AS nmd JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = nmd.network_id AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') JOIN ports_sg_ranked AS psr ON psr.port_id = p.id AND psr.tenant_id = p.tenant_id ORDER BY nmd.network_name, psr.security_group_id, psr.port_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')), AvgStdAttr AS (SELECT AVG(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, (s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID - a.avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr CROSS JOIN AvgStdAttr AS a), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')), IpVersionCounts AS (SELECT s.IP_VERSION, COUNT(*) AS total_subnets FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b') GROUP BY s.IP_VERSION), AverageSubnets AS (SELECT AVG(total_subnets) AS avg_subnets FROM IpVersionCounts) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.window_column, c.IP_VERSION, c.total_subnets, a.avg_subnets AS benchmark, CASE WHEN c.total_subnets > a.avg_subnets * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_SUBNET_USAGE' WHEN c.total_subnets < a.avg_subnets * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_SUBNET_USAGE' ELSE 'AVERAGE_SUBNET_USAGE' END AS usage_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s2.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr JOIN IpVersionCounts AS c ON c.IP_VERSION = s2.IP_VERSION CROSS JOIN AverageSubnets AS a) SELECT q2.IP_VERSION, q2.usage_category, COUNT(*) AS total_filtered_subnets, AVG(q1.deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id) AS avg_deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id, MAX(q2.window_column) AS max_running_sum_standard_attr_id, AVG(q2.benchmark) AS avg_benchmark_subnets_per_ip_version FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.subnet_name = q2.subnet_name AND q1.subnet_cidr = q2.subnet_cidr AND q1.rbac_target_tenant = q2.rbac_target_tenant AND q1.window_column = q2.window_column GROUP BY q2.IP_VERSION, q2.usage_category ORDER BY total_filtered_subnets DESC
WITH DownPortsAboveAvgRunningSum AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), AdminUpPortsAboveVifTypeWindowAvg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON v.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column) SELECT a.PORT_ID, a.HOST, a.VIF_TYPE, a.VNIC_TYPE, a.TENANT_ID, a.NETWORK_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS, a.STATUS, a.DEVICE_OWNER, a.window_column AS admin_up_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM AdminUpPortsAboveVifTypeWindowAvg AS a WHERE a.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') AND a.PORT_ID IN (SELECT port_id FROM DownPortsAboveAvgRunningSum) ORDER BY a.NETWORK_ID, a.PORT_ID

WITH network_mtu_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AverageMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_id, ic.network_name, ic.tenant_id, ic.admin_state_up, END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMtu AS am), port_vif_details AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), AverageVifTypePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM VifTypeCounts) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.total_ports, avp.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN vtc.total_ports > avp.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN vtc.total_ports < avp.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN AverageVifTypePorts AS avp) SELECT nmd.network_id, nmd.network_name, nmd.tenant_id, nmd.admin_state_up, nmd.mtu, nmd.standard_attr_updated_at, nmd.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, nmd.benchmark_mtu, nmd.mtu_category, pvd.PORT_ID, pvd.HOST, pvd.VIF_TYPE, pvd.VNIC_TYPE, pvd.MAC_ADDRESS, pvd.STATUS AS port_status, pvd.DEVICE_OWNER, pvd.window_column AS port_std_attr_id_window_avg, pvd.total_ports AS total_ports_for_vif_type, pvd.benchmark AS avg_ports_per_vif_type_benchmark, pvd.user_category AS vif_type_user_category FROM network_mtu_details AS nmd JOIN port_vif_details AS pvd ON pvd.NETWORK_ID = nmd.network_id AND pvd.TENANT_ID = nmd.tenant_id WHERE nmd.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')
WITH down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), admin_up_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON v.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column) SELECT a.PORT_ID, a.HOST, a.VIF_TYPE, a.VNIC_TYPE, a.TENANT_ID, a.NETWORK_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS, a.STATUS, a.DEVICE_OWNER, a.window_column AS admin_up_windowed_avg_standard_attr_id, d.window_column AS down_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM admin_up_ports AS a JOIN down_ports AS d ON d.port_id = a.PORT_ID ORDER BY a.window_column DESC, d.window_column DESC
WITH subq1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON i.port_id = f.fixed_port_id GROUP BY i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows ORDER BY i.ip_allocation_count DESC LIMIT 10), subq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN WITH down_ports_window AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > 1000), admin_up_ports_bindings AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), AverageBindings AS (SELECT AVG(binding_count) AS avg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.mac_address, ic.binding_count, ic.port_security_enabled_total, ab.avg_binding_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.binding_count > ab.avg_binding_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_BINDINGS' WHEN ic.binding_count < ab.avg_binding_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_BINDINGS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_BINDINGS' END AS binding_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageBindings AS ab), counts AS (SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM down_ports_window) AS down_ports_window_count, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM admin_up_ports_bindings) AS admin_up_ports_bindings_count) SELECT c.down_ports_window_count, c.admin_up_ports_bindings_count FROM counts AS c

WITH up_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), OverallWindowAvg AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS overall_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.ports_in_vif_type, (i.window_column - owa.overall_avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallWindowAvg AS owa), down_ports AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), avg_rank_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_rank FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column AS rank_within_security_group, (i.window_column - a.avg_rank) AS deviation_from_avg_rank FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_rank_cte AS a) SELECT u.PORT_ID, u.HOST, u.VIF_TYPE, u.VNIC_TYPE, u.TENANT_ID, u.NETWORK_ID, u.MAC_ADDRESS, u.STATUS, u.DEVICE_OWNER, u.window_column AS up_ports_window_avg_standard_attr_id, u.ports_in_vif_type, u.deviation_from_overall_window_avg, d.security_group_id, d.rank_within_security_group, d.deviation_from_avg_rank FROM up_ports AS u JOIN down_ports AS d ON u.PORT_ID = d.port_id ORDER BY u.VIF_TYPE, u.PORT_ID
WITH DownPortsRanked AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, security_group_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), AdminUpPortsIpStats AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.ip_allocation_count, inner_cte.subnet_id_rows, COUNT(DISTINCT ia2.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count FROM inner_cte JOIN ipallocations AS ia2 ON ia2.port_id = inner_cte.port_id WHERE NOT ia2.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.ip_allocation_count, inner_cte.subnet_id_rows HAVING inner_cte.ip_allocation_count > 0 AND (CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT ia2.subnet_id) * 1.0 AS REAL) / inner_cte.ip_allocation_count) > 0.75) SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM DownPortsRanked) AS Down_Port_Count_Above_Avg_Rank, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM AdminUpPortsIpStats) AS Admin_Up_Port_Count_With_Distinct_Subnet_Ratio_Above_75pct

WITH PortDnsAndSg AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), AverageDns AS (SELECT AVG(dns_record_count) AS avg_dns_records FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.port_name, ic.security_group_binding_count, ic.dns_record_count, ad.avg_dns_records AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.dns_record_count > ad.avg_dns_records * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DNS' WHEN ic.dns_record_count < ad.avg_dns_records * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DNS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DNS' END AS dns_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageDns AS ad), ActiveNetworksMtu AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), avg_mtu_cte AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, (i.mtu - a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_mtu_cte AS a) SELECT an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column, an.deviation_from_avg_mtu, COUNT(DISTINCT pds.port_id) AS admin_up_sg_bound_port_count, AVG(pds.dns_record_count) AS avg_dns_records_per_port, SUM(CASE WHEN pds.dns_category = 'HIGH_DNS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS high_dns_port_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pds.dns_category = 'LOW_DNS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS low_dns_port_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pds.dns_category = 'AVERAGE_DNS' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS average_dns_port_count FROM ActiveNetworksMtu AS an JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = an.network_id AND p.tenant_id = an.tenant_id AND p.admin_state_up = 1 JOIN PortDnsAndSg AS pds ON pds.port_id = p.id AND pds.tenant_id = an.tenant_id GROUP BY an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column, an.deviation_from_avg_mtu ORDER BY an.tenant_id, an.network_name

ac.avg_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled) AS deviation_from_avg FROM NetworkCounts AS nc CROSS JOIN AverageCounts AS ac) SELECT q1.NAME, q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.MAC_ADDRESS, q1.ADMIN_STATE_UP, q1.STATUS, q1.DEVICE_OWNER, q1.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, q1.window_column, q1.total_ports_per_owner, q1.avg_ports_per_owner, q2.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled, q2.deviation_from_avg FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.NETWORK_ID = q2.network_id
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(security_group_id) OVER (ORDER BY security_group_id, port_id) = security_group_id THEN NULL ELSE security_group_id END AS security_group_id, AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM SecurityGroupPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, WITH max_dest_per_nexthop AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT subnet_name, subnet_cidr, MAX(destination_count) AS max_destinations_per_nexthop FROM (SELECT subnet_name, subnet_cidr, NEXTHOP, COUNT(DISTINCT r.TENANT_ID) = inner_cte.NAME THEN NULL ELSE inner_cte.NAME END AS name, inner_cte.window_column, r.TENANT_ID AS rbac_tenant_id, r.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON n.NAME = inner_cte.NAME JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON r.OBJECT_ID = n.ID AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external' ORDER BY inner_cte.NAME, r.TENANT_ID), sq2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external'), tenant_counts AS (SELECT n.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS tenant_network_count FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external' GROUP BY n.TENANT_ID), avg_counts AS (SELECT AVG(tenant_network_count) AS avg_networks_per_tenant FROM tenant_counts) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, totals.total_matching_networks, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_matching_networks FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external') AS totals ON 1 = 1 JOIN avg_counts ON 1 = 1) SELECT sq1.name, sq1.window_column, sq1.rbac_tenant_id, sq1.rbac_target_tenant, sq2.total_matching_networks, sq2.avg_networks_per_tenant, ROUND(CAST(sq2.total_matching_networks AS REAL) / NULLIF(sq2.avg_networks_per_tenant, 0), 2) AS total_to_avg_ratio FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq2.NAME = sq1.name AND sq2.window_column = sq1.window_column ORDER BY sq1.name, sq1.rbac_tenant_id
WITH networks_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.network_id, inner_cte.network_name, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.admin_state_up, inner_cte.mtu, inner_cte.standard_attr_updated_at, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_active_networks, avg_counts.avg_active_networks FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS total_active_networks FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(active_network_count) AS avg_active_networks FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS active_network_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1), ports_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, COUNT(DISTINCT ia2.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count, (i.ip_allocation_count - a.avg_ip_allocation_count) AS deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.id = i.port_id JOIN ipallocations AS ia2 ON ia2.port_id = p2.id CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a WHERE NOT ia2.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, a.avg_ip_allocation_count) SELECT n.network_id, n.network_name, n.tenant_id, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at AS network_standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, n.total_active_networks, n.avg_active_networks, p.port_id, p.mac_address, p.ip_allocation_count, p.distinct_subnet_count, p.deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations FROM networks_subq AS n JOIN ports AS prt ON prt.network_id = n.network_id AND prt.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') JOIN ports_subq AS p ON p.port_id = prt.id AND p.tenant_id = n.tenant_id ORDER BY n.tenant_id, n.network_name, p.port_id

FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, security_group_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)), standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = total_active_networks FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(active_network_count) AS avg_active_networks FROM (SELECT pr.port_id = p.id AND pr.tenant_id = an.tenant_id ORDER BY an.total_active_networks DESC, an.network_name, pr.security_group_id, pr.port_id
WITH port_dns_security AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), AverageDns AS (SELECT AVG(dns_record_count) AS avg_dns_records FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.port_name, ic.security_group_binding_count, ic.dns_record_count, ad.avg_dns_records AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.dns_record_count > ad.avg_dns_records * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DNS' WHEN ic.dns_record_count < ad.avg_dns_records * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DNS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DNS' END AS dns_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageDns AS ad), active_network_mtu AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), avg_mtu_cte AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.window_column, (i.mtu - a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_mtu_cte AS a), port_network_bridge AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.network_id FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT anm.network_id, anm.network_name, anm.tenant_id, anm.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, anm.mtu, anm.standard_attr_updated_at, anm.window_column, anm.deviation_from_avg_mtu, pds.port_id, pds.port_name, pds.security_group_binding_count, pds.dns_record_count, pds.benchmark AS avg_dns_records_benchmark, pds.dns_category FROM active_network_mtu AS anm JOIN port_network_bridge AS pnb ON pnb.network_id = anm.network_id JOIN port_dns_security AS pds ON pds.port_id = pnb.port_id WHERE anm.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')
WITH q1 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupPorts AS (SELECT security_group_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM SecurityGroupPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, sgp.total_ports AS total_down_ports_in_security_group, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN sgp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN sgp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SecurityGroupPorts AS sgp ON i.security_group_id = sgp.security_group_id CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap), q2 AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AverageStdAttr AS (SELECT AVG(p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = p2.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb2 ON p2.ID = psb2.PORT_ID WHERE p2.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p2.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, (p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID - a.avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = i.port_id CROSS JOIN AverageStdAttr AS a) SELECT q1.port_id, q1.tenant_id, q1.mac_address, q1.security_group_id, q1.port_rank_within_security_group, q1.total_down_ports_in_security_group, q1.benchmark AS avg_down_ports_benchmark_across_security_groups, q1.user_category, q2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, q2.deviation_from_avg_standard_attr_id FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.port_id = q2.port_id AND q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id AND q1.mac_address = q2.mac_address

a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_mtu_cte AS a), ports_subq AS (WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), OverallWindowAvg AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS overall_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.ports_in_vif_type, (i.window_column - owa.overall_avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallWindowAvg AS owa) SELECT n.network_id, n.network_name, n.tenant_id, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant_by_name, n.deviation_from_avg_mtu, p.PORT_ID, p.HOST, p.VIF_TYPE, p.VNIC_TYPE, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER, p.window_column AS port_windowed_avg_standard_attr_id, p.ports_in_vif_type, p.deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM networks_subq AS n JOIN ports_subq AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id AND p.TENANT_ID = n.tenant_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_down_ports, avg_down.avg_down_port_count FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_port_count FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id, avg_down.avg_down_port_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT inner_cte.loadbalancer_name, inner_cte.vip_address, inner_cte.bytes_in, inner_cte.total_connections, inner_cte.window_column, lb.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, tenant_counts.haproxy_lb_count AS tenant_haproxy_loadbalancer_count, avg_counts.avg_haproxy_lb_count AS avg_haproxy_loadbalancers_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.NAME = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS = inner_cte.vip_address JOIN (SELECT lb2.TENANT_ID, COUNT(lb2.ID) AS haproxy_lb_count FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb2.TENANT_ID) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.TENANT_ID = lb.TENANT_ID JOIN (SELECT AVG(sub.haproxy_lb_count) AS avg_haproxy_lb_count FROM (SELECT lb3.TENANT_ID, COUNT(lb3.ID) AS haproxy_lb_count FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb3 JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra3 ON lb3.ID = pra3.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra3.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb3.TENANT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS active_shared_rbac_networks FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT n.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.id) AS total_active_shared_networks, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN r.target_tenant <> '*' THEN r.id END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_specific_target_tenant_share FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.tenant_id ORDER BY total_active_shared_networks DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS online_haproxy_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' AND pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), TenantCounts AS (SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS tenant_online_haproxy_count FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' AND pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID), AvgTenantCount AS (SELECT AVG(tenant_online_haproxy_count) AS avg_tenant_online_haproxy_count FROM TenantCounts) SELECT tc.TENANT_ID, tc.tenant_online_haproxy_count FROM TenantCounts AS tc CROSS JOIN inner_cte CROSS JOIN AvgTenantCount AS atc WHERE tc.tenant_online_haproxy_count > atc.avg_tenant_online_haproxy_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, SUM(1) AS total_security_bindings FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.id = nsb.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.name HAVING SUM(1) > 1) SELECT MAX(total_security_bindings) AS max_total_security_bindings FROM inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, (i.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), HostCounts AS (SELECT host, COUNT(DISTINCT ip_address) AS down_allocated_ips FROM inner_cte GROUP BY host), AverageHostCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_allocated_ips) AS avg_down_allocated_ips FROM HostCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, hc.down_allocated_ips, (hc.down_allocated_ips - ahc.avg_down_allocated_ips) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN HostCounts AS hc ON i.host = hc.host CROSS JOIN AverageHostCounts AS ahc
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), NetworkRbacCounts AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, COUNT(r.id) AS total_rbacs FROM networks AS n JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.id) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.name AND n.status = i.status AND n.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up JOIN NetworkRbacCounts AS c ON c.network_id = n.id WHERE c.total_rbacs > (SELECT AVG(total_rbacs) FROM NetworkRbacCounts)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT NAME, NETWORK_ID, MAC_ADDRESS, ADMIN_STATE_UP, STATUS, DEVICE_OWNER, SECURITY_GROUP_ID, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE SECURITY_GROUP_ID IN (SELECT SECURITY_GROUP_ID FROM inner_cte GROUP BY SECURITY_GROUP_ID HAVING COUNT(*) > 5)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC), NetworkSecurityGroupGeoMean AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID JOIN inner_cte AS i ON i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT NETWORK_ID, MAX(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS max_security_group_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM NetworkSecurityGroupGeoMean GROUP BY NETWORK_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), NetworkDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID) SELECT network_id, total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM NetworkDownSecurePorts WHERE total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled > (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled) FROM NetworkDownSecurePorts)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_security_enabled_total) AS avg_enabled_total FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.binding_count, i.port_security_enabled_total, (i.port_security_enabled_total - a.avg_enabled_total) AS deviation_from_avg_enabled_total FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT subnet_name, subnet_cidr, rbac_target_tenant, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT p.STATUS, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up, inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON 1 = 1 JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY p.STATUS, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up, inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id HAVING inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id > (SELECT AVG(p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) FROM PORTS AS p2 JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b2 ON p2.ID = b2.PORT_ID WHERE b2.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs')
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.TENANT_ID, tdsp.down_secure_ports, adsp.avg_down_secure_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports > adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdsp.down_secure_ports < adsp.avg_down_secure_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS overall_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageDownSecurePorts AS adsp CROSS JOIN inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = inner_cte.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.ID > (SELECT AVG(sa3.ID) FROM PORTS AS p3 JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa3 ON sa3.ID = p3.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p3.STATUS = 'DOWN')
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up, inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.STATUS = p.STATUS JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY p.STATUS, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up, inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id HAVING inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id > (SELECT AVG(p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) FROM PORTS AS p2 JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b2 ON p2.ID = b2.PORT_ID WHERE b2.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs')
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT inner_cte.gateway_ip, MAX(inner_cte.max_ip_version) AS max_ip_version_across_subnets FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.name = inner_cte.name AND s2.gateway_ip = inner_cte.gateway_ip WHERE s2.enable_dhcp = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(inner_cte.max_ip_version) > 3
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), ProviderAverages AS (SELECT pra.PROVIDER_NAME, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID GROUP BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME) SELECT i.loadbalancer_name, i.vip_address, i.bytes_in, i.total_connections, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 ON lb2.NAME = i.loadbalancer_name AND lb2.VIP_ADDRESS = i.vip_address JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE i.total_connections > (SELECT avg_total_connections FROM ProviderAverages WHERE ProviderAverages.PROVIDER_NAME = pra2.PROVIDER_NAME) ORDER BY i.loadbalancer_name
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT security_group_id, MAX(window_column) AS max_port_rank FROM inner_cte GROUP BY security_group_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT NAME, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), VifDownCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), AverageDownCounts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM VifDownCounts) SELECT i.NAME, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column, vdc.total_down_ports, adc.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN vdc.total_down_ports > adc.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DOWN' WHEN vdc.total_down_ports < adc.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DOWN' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DOWN' END AS down_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifDownCounts AS vdc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vdc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN AverageDownCounts AS adc ORDER BY i.MAC_ADDRESS
