WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_id, ic.tenant_id, ic.mac_address, ic.ip_allocation_count, ic.subnet_id_rows, (ic.ip_allocation_count - a.avg_ip_allocation_count) AS deviation_from_avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.MAC_ADDRESS = p.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT tdp.tenant_id, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM TenantDownPorts AS tdp CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, COUNT(*) AS total_networks, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_action FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(action_count) AS avg_networks_per_action FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS action_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY window_column) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_action
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) SELECT i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_loadbalancers FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_haproxy_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') AS totals ON 1 = 1 WHERE (CAST(i.total_loadbalancers * 1.0 AS REAL) / totals.total_haproxy_loadbalancers) > 0.50
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), NetworkPortCounts AS (SELECT p.network_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS ports_in_network FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.network_id, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT npc.port_status, npc.device_owner, MAX(npc.ports_in_network) AS max_ports_in_any_network FROM NetworkPortCounts AS npc JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.port_status = npc.port_status AND ic.device_owner = npc.device_owner GROUP BY npc.port_status, npc.device_owner
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up, SUM(inner_cte.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings, SUM(inner_cte.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_with_sg_binding_admin_up_per_tenant FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id, avg_ports.avg_port_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS) SELECT i.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports, AVG(i.window_column) AS avg_window_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b2 ON NOT i.MAC_ADDRESS IS NULL AND i.VIF_TYPE = b2.VIF_TYPE GROUP BY i.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), avg_rank AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.NAME, i.window_column, a.avg_window_column AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.window_column > a.avg_window_column * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_RANK' WHEN i.window_column < a.avg_window_column * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_RANK' ELSE 'AVERAGE_RANK' END AS rank_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_rank AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.name AS loadbalancer_name, MAX(lb.admin_state_up) AS max_admin_state_up, lb.operating_status FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.id = pra.resource_id WHERE pra.provider_name = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.name, lb.operating_status HAVING MAX(lb.admin_state_up) = 1) SELECT lb.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT lb.id) AS total_qualifying_loadbalancers, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN inner_cte.operating_status = 'ONLINE' THEN lb.id END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_online FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.name = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.operating_status = inner_cte.operating_status GROUP BY lb.tenant_id ORDER BY total_qualifying_loadbalancers DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 3) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(inner_cte.network_id) OVER (ORDER BY inner_cte.network_id) = inner_cte.network_id THEN NULL ELSE inner_cte.network_id END AS network_id, inner_cte.subnet_count, s.TENANT_ID AS subnet_tenant_id FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = inner_cte.network_id ORDER BY inner_cte.network_id, s.TENANT_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1), TenantDownSecurePorts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_secure_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AverageTenantCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_secure_ports) AS avg_down_secure_ports FROM TenantDownSecurePorts) SELECT tdsp.tenant_id, tdsp.down_secure_ports, (tdsp.down_secure_ports - atc.avg_down_secure_ports) AS deviation_from_avg, inner_cte.down_ports_with_port_security_enabled AS total_down_ports_with_port_security_enabled FROM TenantDownSecurePorts AS tdsp CROSS JOIN AverageTenantCounts AS atc CROSS JOIN inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY p.STATUS), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(avg_standard_attr_id) AS overall_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id, (i.avg_standard_attr_id - o.overall_avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_overall_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS o
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SubnetRouteCounts AS (SELECT s.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(r.destination) AS route_count FROM subnets AS s LEFT JOIN subnetroutes AS r ON r.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 GROUP BY s.id), AverageRouteCounts AS (SELECT AVG(route_count) AS avg_route_count FROM SubnetRouteCounts) SELECT ic.DESTINATION, ic.NEXTHOP, ic.subnet_name, ic.subnet_cidr, ic.FIRST_IP, ic.LAST_IP, ic.window_column, src.route_count, (src.route_count - arc.avg_route_count) AS deviation_from_avg_route_count FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN subnets AS s ON s.cidr = ic.subnet_cidr AND s.name = ic.subnet_name JOIN SubnetRouteCounts AS src ON src.subnet_id = s.id CROSS JOIN AverageRouteCounts AS arc
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS), TenantPorts AS (SELECT p2.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(p2.ID) AS total_ports FROM PORTS AS p2 GROUP BY p2.TENANT_ID), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM TenantPorts) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.window_column, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, tp.total_ports, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = i.port_id JOIN TenantPorts AS tp ON tp.tenant_id = p.TENANT_ID CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS) SELECT inner_cte.allowed_mac_address, inner_cte.allowed_ip_address, inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.security_group_id, inner_cte.window_column, port_sg_counts.total_sg_bindings_on_port, avg_sg.avg_sg_bindings_per_port FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT sgpb.PORT_ID AS port_id, COUNT(*) AS total_sg_bindings_on_port FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb GROUP BY sgpb.PORT_ID) AS port_sg_counts ON port_sg_counts.port_id = inner_cte.port_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(sg_count) AS avg_sg_bindings_per_port FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS sg_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS GROUP BY PORT_ID) AS sub) AS avg_sg ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.allowed_ip_address
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ResourceTypeAverages AS (SELECT sa.RESOURCE_TYPE, AVG(sa.ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.ID > (SELECT avg_standard_attr_id FROM ResourceTypeAverages AS rta WHERE rta.RESOURCE_TYPE = sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), haproxy_avg AS (SELECT AVG(stats2.BYTES_IN * 1.0) AS avg_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats2 JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 ON stats2.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb2.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra2 ON lb2.ID = pra2.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra2.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT i.loadbalancer_name, i.vip_address, i.bytes_in, i.total_connections, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN haproxy_avg AS a WHERE i.bytes_in > a.avg_bytes_in ORDER BY i.loadbalancer_name
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS) SELECT allowed_mac_address, allowed_ip_address, port_id, security_group_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > 0 ORDER BY allowed_ip_address
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(total_loadbalancers) AS avg_loadbalancers FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_loadbalancers, a.avg_loadbalancers AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.total_loadbalancers > a.avg_loadbalancers * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_COUNT' WHEN i.total_loadbalancers < a.avg_loadbalancers * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_COUNT' ELSE 'AVERAGE_COUNT' END AS count_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, SUM(1) AS total_security_bindings FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb ON n.id = nsb.network_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.name HAVING SUM(1) > 1), AverageBindings AS (SELECT AVG(total_security_bindings) AS avg_bindings FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_name, ic.total_security_bindings, ab.avg_bindings AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.total_security_bindings > ab.avg_bindings * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_BINDINGS' WHEN ic.total_security_bindings < ab.avg_bindings * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_BINDINGS' ELSE 'AVERAGE_BINDINGS' END AS bindings_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageBindings AS ab
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(max_ip_version) AS avg_max_ip_version, COUNT(*) AS total_groups FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version, a.total_groups, (i.max_ip_version - a.avg_max_ip_version) AS deviation_from_avg_max_ip_version FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), AdminUpUtilization AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, SUM(CASE WHEN p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS admin_up_ports, COUNT(p.ID) AS total_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN AdminUpUtilization AS a ON a.port_status = i.port_status AND a.device_owner = i.device_owner WHERE a.total_ports > 0 AND (CAST(a.admin_up_ports * 1.0 AS REAL) / a.total_ports) >= 0.80
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.min_standard_attr_id, avg_counts.avg_port_count FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) AS avg_counts
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS, stats.BYTES_IN, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY lb.TENANT_ID ORDER BY lb.ID) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON lb.ID = stats.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), tenant_avg AS (SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON lb.ID = stats.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID) SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, i.NAME, i.VIP_ADDRESS, i.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS, i.BYTES_IN, i.window_column, ta.avg_total_connections AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS > ta.avg_total_connections * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_CONN' WHEN i.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS < ta.avg_total_connections * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_CONN' ELSE 'AVG_CONN' END AS connection_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.NAME = i.NAME AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS = i.VIP_ADDRESS JOIN tenant_avg AS ta ON ta.TENANT_ID = lb.TENANT_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id AS ports_tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations, SUM(inner_cte.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, SUM(inner_cte.subnet_id_rows) AS total_subnet_id_rows, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_per_tenant FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(port_id) AS port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id, avg_ports.avg_port_count
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS active_shared_rbac_networks FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared') SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, COUNT(DISTINCT r.TARGET_TENANT) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID CROSS JOIN inner_cte WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.ID, n.NAME HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT r.TARGET_TENANT) > 2
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), NetworkAverages AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, AVG(inner_cte.window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.ID GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.ip_address, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, inner_cte.window_column, na.avg_window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.ID JOIN NetworkAverages AS na ON p.NETWORK_ID = na.network_id WHERE inner_cte.window_column > na.avg_window_column
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb2.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_security_group_bound_ports FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.STATUS = i.port_status AND p2.DEVICE_OWNER = i.device_owner LEFT JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb2 ON p2.ID = spb2.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.port_count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), VifTypePorts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(PORT_ID) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM VifTypePorts) SELECT vtp.VIF_TYPE, vtp.total_ports, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN vtp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN vtp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM VifTypePorts AS vtp CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_sg_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_sg_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(port_id) AS total_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT tenant_id, total_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts WHERE total_down_ports > (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) FROM TenantDownPorts)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 3), AverageSubnets AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnet_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_id, ic.subnet_count, a.avg_subnet_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.subnet_count > a.avg_subnet_count * 1.5 THEN 'ABOVE_BENCHMARK' WHEN ic.subnet_count < a.avg_subnet_count * 0.5 THEN 'BELOW_BENCHMARK' ELSE 'NEAR_BENCHMARK' END AS subnet_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageSubnets AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT i.NAME, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s2 ON s2.TENANT_ID = i.TENANT_ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p2 ON p2.SUBNET_ID = s2.ID WHERE i.FIRST_IP = (SELECT MIN(p3.FIRST_IP) FROM IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p3 WHERE p3.SUBNET_ID = s2.ID)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.ID AS loadbalancer_id, lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE'), provider_avg AS (SELECT pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE' GROUP BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME) SELECT i.loadbalancer_id, i.loadbalancer_name, i.provider_name, i.vip_address, i.window_column AS rank_within_provider_by_total_connections, (stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS - pa.avg_total_connections) AS deviation_from_provider_avg_total_connections FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = i.loadbalancer_id JOIN provider_avg AS pa ON pa.provider_name = i.provider_name
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS) SELECT VIF_TYPE, MAX(window_column) AS max_windowed_avg_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY lb.TENANT_ID ORDER BY lb.ID) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), ProviderAverages AS (SELECT provider_name, AVG(total_connections) AS avg_total_connections FROM inner_cte GROUP BY provider_name) SELECT i.loadbalancer_name, i.vip_address, i.bytes_in, i.total_connections, i.provider_name, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ProviderAverages AS pa ON i.provider_name = pa.provider_name WHERE i.total_connections > pa.avg_total_connections
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT port_status, device_owner, port_count, min_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE port_count > (SELECT AVG(port_count) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE'), rbac_counts AS (SELECT n.id AS network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT r.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY n.id) SELECT i.name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.action, i.target_tenant, c.distinct_target_tenants, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN rbac_counts AS c ON c.network_id = (SELECT n2.id FROM networks AS n2 WHERE n2.name = i.name AND n2.status = i.status AND n2.admin_state_up = i.admin_state_up LIMIT 1)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), DhcpAverages AS (SELECT ENABLE_DHCP, AVG(max_standard_attr_id_next2) AS avg_max_next2 FROM inner_cte GROUP BY ENABLE_DHCP) SELECT i.NAME, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN DhcpAverages AS d ON i.ENABLE_DHCP = d.ENABLE_DHCP WHERE i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 > d.avg_max_next2
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS, stats.BYTES_IN, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY lb.TENANT_ID ORDER BY lb.ID) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON lb.ID = stats.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.VIP_ADDRESS, inner_cte.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS, inner_cte.BYTES_IN, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_avg.avg_total_connections FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON lb.ID = stats.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID) AS tenant_avg ON tenant_avg.TENANT_ID = (SELECT lb2.TENANT_ID FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb2 WHERE lb2.VIP_ADDRESS = inner_cte.VIP_ADDRESS LIMIT 1)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), HostVifShare AS (SELECT host, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS down_ports, SUM(CASE WHEN vif_type = 'ovs' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS ovs_down_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY host) SELECT host, down_ports, ovs_down_ports, (CAST(ovs_down_ports * 1.0 AS REAL) / down_ports) AS ovs_share FROM HostVifShare WHERE down_ports > 0 AND (CAST(ovs_down_ports * 1.0 AS REAL) / down_ports) > 0.60
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy') SELECT lb.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, MAX(inner_cte.total_connections) AS max_total_connections FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.NAME = inner_cte.loadbalancer_name AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS = inner_cte.vip_address GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 3), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnet_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_id, i.subnet_count, (i.subnet_count - a.avg_subnet_count) AS deviation_from_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.PORT_ID) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT psb.PORT_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_port_security_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON i.PORT_ID = psb.PORT_ID GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_ports DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC) SELECT SECURITY_GROUP_ID, geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE geometric_mean_standard_attr_id > 1000 ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), SecurityGroupDownCounts AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_group FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID), AverageDownCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_in_group) AS avg_down_ports_in_group FROM SecurityGroupDownCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, sgdc.down_ports_in_group, (sgdc.down_ports_in_group - adc.avg_down_ports_in_group) AS deviation_from_avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.PORT_ID = i.port_id JOIN SecurityGroupDownCounts AS sgdc ON sgdc.security_group_id = sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID CROSS JOIN AverageDownCounts AS adc
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT i.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT i.NETWORK_ID) AS total_default_external_networks, MIN(i.MTU) AS min_mtu_in_tenant FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.TENANT_ID ORDER BY total_default_external_networks DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.device_id IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7')), TenantPorts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(inner_cte.port_id) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = inner_cte.port_id GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM TenantPorts) SELECT tp.tenant_id, tp.total_ports, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tp.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tp.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM TenantPorts AS tp CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_down_ports_for_tenant, avg_counts.avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT p.tenant_id AS tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports_for_tenant FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_counts.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM (SELECT COUNT(p.id) AS port_count FROM ports AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.network_id, inner_cte.network_name, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.admin_state_up, inner_cte.mtu, inner_cte.standard_attr_updated_at, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_active_networks, avg_counts.avg_active_networks FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS total_active_networks FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(active_network_count) AS avg_active_networks FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(network_id) AS active_network_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON inner_cte.PORT_ID = b.PORT_ID GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000), TargetTenantCounts AS (SELECT ic.network_name, ic.rbac_action, ic.max_mtu, COUNT(DISTINCT r.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON n.name = ic.network_name JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON r.object_id = n.id AND r.action = ic.rbac_action GROUP BY ic.network_name, ic.rbac_action, ic.max_mtu), AverageTargetTenantCounts AS (SELECT AVG(distinct_target_tenants) AS avg_distinct_target_tenants FROM TargetTenantCounts) SELECT ttc.network_name, ttc.rbac_action, ttc.max_mtu, ttc.distinct_target_tenants, (ttc.distinct_target_tenants - atc.avg_distinct_target_tenants) AS deviation_from_avg_distinct_target_tenants FROM TargetTenantCounts AS ttc CROSS JOIN AverageTargetTenantCounts AS atc
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.min_standard_attr_id, avg_counts.avg_port_count FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb2.security_group_id) > COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_multi_sg_bindings FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.port_status AND p2.device_owner = i.device_owner AND p2.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb2 ON p2.id = spb2.port_id GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.port_count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), TenantPortCounts AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = ic.NETWORK_ID AND p.MAC_ADDRESS = ic.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.DEVICE_OWNER = ic.DEVICE_OWNER GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID), AveragePorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM TenantPortCounts) SELECT tpc.TENANT_ID, tpc.total_ports, ap.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tpc.total_ports > ap.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tpc.total_ports < ap.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM TenantPortCounts AS tpc CROSS JOIN AveragePorts AS ap
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > 1000
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, port_name, security_group_binding_count, dns_record_count FROM inner_cte WHERE security_group_binding_count > (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')), PortCounts AS (SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.PORT_ID = b.PORT_ID GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE), AvgWindowByVif AS (SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(ic.window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN inner_cte AS ic ON ic.PORT_ID = b.PORT_ID GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE) SELECT ic.PORT_ID, ic.TENANT_ID, ic.MAC_ADDRESS, ic.window_column, pc.ports_in_vif_type, (ic.window_column - aw.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_vif_avg FROM inner_cte AS ic JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = ic.PORT_ID JOIN PortCounts AS pc ON pc.VIF_TYPE = b.VIF_TYPE JOIN AvgWindowByVif AS aw ON aw.VIF_TYPE = b.VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000) SELECT inner_cte.rbac_action, MAX(inner_cte.max_mtu) AS highest_network_max_mtu FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.rbac_action
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, stats.BYTES_IN AS bytes_in, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY lb.NAME ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' AND lb.VIP_ADDRESS IN ('10.20.127.222/8')) SELECT MAX(bytes_in) AS max_bytes_in FROM inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT i.status, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.id) AS total_ports, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p2 ON p2.status = i.status JOIN ml2_port_bindings AS b2 ON p2.id = b2.port_id WHERE b2.vif_type = 'ovs' AND p2.device_id IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY i.status, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id ORDER BY total_ports DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND lb.VIP_SUBNET_ID IN (SELECT subnets.id FROM subnets JOIN networks ON networks.id = subnets.network_id WHERE NOT networks.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_bytes_in, CASE WHEN MAX(CASE WHEN s.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS > 10000 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_lb_over_10000_total_connections FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.OPERATING_STATUS = i.OPERATING_STATUS JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON s.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_bytes_in ORDER BY i.total_bytes_in DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT STATUS, min_admin_state_up, avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE avg_standard_attr_id > (SELECT AVG(avg_standard_attr_id) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT inner_cte.subnet_name, inner_cte.subnet_cidr, inner_cte.rbac_target_tenant, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_counts.total_subnets_for_target_tenant, avg_counts.avg_subnets_per_target_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS total_subnets_for_target_tenant FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS tenant_counts ON tenant_counts.rbac_target_tenant = inner_cte.rbac_target_tenant, (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_subnets_per_target_tenant FROM (SELECT rbac_target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY rbac_target_tenant) AS sub) AS avg_counts
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT p.network_id, MAX(inner_cte.security_group_binding_count) AS max_security_group_bindings_on_a_port, MAX(inner_cte.dns_record_count) AS max_dns_records_on_a_port FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON inner_cte.port_id = p.id GROUP BY p.network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC) SELECT SECURITY_GROUP_ID, geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE geometric_mean_standard_attr_id > 1000 ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, CASE WHEN NOT sa.DESCRIPTION IS NULL AND sa.DESCRIPTION <> '' THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_description FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = inner_cte.NAME JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT b.VIF_TYPE, MAX(inner_cte.min_admin_state_up) AS max_of_min_admin_state_up, MAX(inner_cte.avg_standard_attr_id) AS max_of_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.STATUS = inner_cte.STATUS JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AverageMtu AS (SELECT AVG(mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.network_id, ic.network_name, ic.tenant_id, ic.admin_state_up, ic.mtu, ic.standard_attr_updated_at, ic.window_column, am.avg_mtu AS benchmark_mtu, CASE WHEN ic.mtu > am.avg_mtu * 1.1 THEN 'HIGH_MTU' WHEN ic.mtu < am.avg_mtu * 0.9 THEN 'LOW_MTU' ELSE 'AVERAGE_MTU' END AS mtu_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN AverageMtu AS am
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, inner_cte.window_column FROM inner_cte JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON inner_cte.port_id = sgpb.PORT_ID WHERE inner_cte.window_column > 100 ORDER BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.window_column
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external') SELECT n.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.id) AS total_qualifying_networks, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN r.target_tenant <> '*' THEN r.id END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_targeted_rbac FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.name JOIN externalnetworks AS e ON e.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON r.object_id = n.id WHERE e.is_default = 0 AND r.action = 'access_as_external' GROUP BY n.tenant_id ORDER BY total_qualifying_networks DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' AND n.target_tenant = '*' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT inner_cte.name, inner_cte.gateway_ip, inner_cte.max_ip_version, avg_maxes.avg_max_ip_version FROM inner_cte, (SELECT AVG(max_ip_version) AS avg_max_ip_version FROM inner_cte) AS avg_maxes
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupAverages AS (SELECT AVG(sg_port_count) AS avg_ports_per_sg FROM (SELECT sgpb2.security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS sg_port_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON sgpb2.port_id = p2.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb2 ON p2.id = psb2.port_id GROUP BY sgpb2.security_group_id) AS sg_counts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT sgpb3.security_group_id, COUNT(*) AS total_ports_in_sg FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb3 JOIN PORTS AS p3 ON sgpb3.port_id = p3.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb3 ON p3.id = psb3.port_id GROUP BY sgpb3.security_group_id) AS sg_totals ON sg_totals.security_group_id = i.security_group_id WHERE sg_totals.total_ports_in_sg > (SELECT avg_ports_per_sg FROM SecurityGroupAverages)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), TenantAverages AS (SELECT tenant_id, AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocs FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantAverages AS ta ON ta.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE i.ip_allocation_count > ta.avg_ip_allocs
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT p.network_id, MAX(inner_cte.ip_allocation_count) AS max_ip_allocations_on_a_port FROM inner_cte JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = inner_cte.port_id GROUP BY p.network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND lb.VIP_SUBNET_ID IN (SELECT subnets.id FROM subnets JOIN networks ON networks.id = subnets.network_id WHERE NOT networks.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT lb.TENANT_ID, MAX(inner_cte.total_bytes_in) AS max_total_bytes_in_across_statuses FROM inner_cte JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON lb.OPERATING_STATUS = inner_cte.OPERATING_STATUS WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.TENANT_ID HAVING MAX(inner_cte.total_bytes_in) > 1000000
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' AND n.target_tenant = '*' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(name) OVER (ORDER BY name, gateway_ip) = name THEN NULL ELSE name END AS name, max_ip_version, gateway_ip FROM inner_cte ORDER BY name, gateway_ip
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), TenantAvg AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.ID = i.port_id JOIN TenantAvg AS ta ON ta.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > ta.avg_standard_attr_id
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.security_group_id, inner_cte.window_column, tenant_down.total_down_ports_for_tenant, avg_down.avg_down_ports_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports_for_tenant FROM ports WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_down ON inner_cte.tenant_id = tenant_down.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports_per_tenant FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM ports WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_down ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.port_status AND p.device_owner = i.device_owner JOIN standardattributes AS sa ON sa.id = p.standard_attr_id LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON p.id = f.fixed_port_id WHERE sa.resource_type = 'ports' AND NOT p.network_id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY i.port_count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT IN ('1ee6b5b4fe8c45cfa16c3ecdbb8ee02b')) SELECT s.IP_VERSION, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = inner_cte.subnet_name AND s.CIDR = inner_cte.subnet_cidr GROUP BY s.IP_VERSION
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' AND n.TARGET_TENANT = '*' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 3) SELECT i.network_id, i.subnet_count, COUNT(DISTINCT s.TENANT_ID) AS distinct_subnet_tenants FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NETWORK_ID = i.network_id GROUP BY i.network_id, i.subnet_count ORDER BY i.subnet_count DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT i.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_down_ports, MIN(i.port_id) AS sample_port_id, MIN(i.tenant_id) AS tenant_id, MIN(i.mac_address) AS sample_mac_address, MIN(i.window_column) AS best_rank_in_group FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 ON sgpb2.security_group_id = i.security_group_id JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.id = sgpb2.port_id GROUP BY i.security_group_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, avg_groups.avg_port_count_across_groups FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_across_groups FROM inner_cte) AS avg_groups ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS v ON v.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE WHERE i.window_column > v.avg_window_column
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' AND n.target_tenant = '*' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version, CASE WHEN MAX(s.enable_dhcp) = 1 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_dhcp_enabled FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.name = i.name AND s.gateway_ip = i.gateway_ip GROUP BY i.name, i.gateway_ip, i.max_ip_version ORDER BY i.max_ip_version DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON i.port_id = f.fixed_port_id GROUP BY i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows ORDER BY i.ip_allocation_count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), VifTypeCounts AS (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS ports_in_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE), OverallWindowAvg AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS overall_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, vtc.ports_in_vif_type, (i.window_column - owa.overall_avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_overall_window_avg FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeCounts AS vtc ON i.VIF_TYPE = vtc.VIF_TYPE CROSS JOIN OverallWindowAvg AS owa
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external'), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(window_column) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.NAME, i.window_column, (i.window_column - a.avg_window_column) AS deviation_from_avg_window_column FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, security_group_id, window_column FROM inner_cte WHERE window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, (i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id - a.avg_geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_avg_geometric_mean FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT ic.port_status, ic.device_owner, ic.port_count, ic.min_standard_attr_id, ac.avg_port_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN ic.port_count > ac.avg_port_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_GROUP' WHEN ic.port_count < ac.avg_port_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_GROUP' ELSE 'AVERAGE_GROUP' END AS group_category FROM inner_cte AS ic CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS ac
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteCounts AS (SELECT s.ID AS subnet_id, COUNT(r.DESTINATION) AS total_routes FROM SUBNETS AS s LEFT JOIN SUBNETROUTES AS r ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 GROUP BY s.ID), AverageRoutes AS (SELECT AVG(total_routes) AS avg_routes FROM SubnetRouteCounts) SELECT i.DESTINATION, i.NEXTHOP, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.window_column, src.total_routes, ar.avg_routes AS benchmark, CASE WHEN src.total_routes > ar.avg_routes * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_ROUTE_SUBNET' WHEN src.total_routes < ar.avg_routes * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_ROUTE_SUBNET' ELSE 'AVERAGE_ROUTE_SUBNET' END AS route_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s.CIDR = i.subnet_cidr JOIN SubnetRouteCounts AS src ON src.subnet_id = s.ID CROSS JOIN AverageRoutes AS ar
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external'), tenant_counts AS (SELECT n.TENANT_ID, COUNT(*) AS tenant_network_count FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external' GROUP BY n.TENANT_ID), avg_counts AS (SELECT AVG(tenant_network_count) AS avg_networks_per_tenant FROM tenant_counts) SELECT inner_cte.NAME, inner_cte.window_column, totals.total_matching_networks, avg_counts.avg_networks_per_tenant FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_matching_networks FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND r.ACTION = 'access_as_external') AS totals ON 1 = 1 JOIN avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')), TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS total_down_ports FROM ports AS p JOIN standardattributes AS sa ON sa.id = p.standard_attr_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AverageDownPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPorts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.window_column, tdp.total_down_ports, adp.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tdp.total_down_ports > adp.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tdp.total_down_ports < adp.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPorts AS tdp ON tdp.tenant_id = i.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageDownPorts AS adp
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC) SELECT SECURITY_GROUP_ID, geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte WHERE geometric_mean_standard_attr_id > (SELECT AVG(geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(port_security_enabled_total) AS avg_enabled_total FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.binding_count, i.port_security_enabled_total, (i.port_security_enabled_total - a.avg_enabled_total) AS deviation_from_avg_enabled_total FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT s.NETWORK_ID, MAX(inner_cte.window_column) AS max_window_dense_rank, MIN(inner_cte.NAME) AS example_subnet_name FROM inner_cte JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = inner_cte.NAME GROUP BY s.NETWORK_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.binding_count, i.port_security_enabled_total, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT f.id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_floating_ips FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN floatingips AS f ON i.port_id = f.fixed_port_id GROUP BY i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.binding_count, i.port_security_enabled_total
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC) SELECT i.security_group_id, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN p.admin_state_up = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_admin_up_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS s ON i.security_group_id = s.security_group_id JOIN ports AS p ON p.id = s.port_id WHERE p.standard_attr_id > 0 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY i.security_group_id, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(avg_standard_attr_id) AS overall_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.STATUS, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id, (i.avg_standard_attr_id - o.overall_avg_standard_attr_id) AS deviation_from_overall_avg FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS o
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT subnet_name, subnet_cidr, MAX(destination_count) AS max_destinations_per_nexthop FROM (SELECT subnet_name, subnet_cidr, NEXTHOP, COUNT(DISTINCT DESTINATION) AS destination_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY subnet_name, subnet_cidr, NEXTHOP) AS t GROUP BY subnet_name, subnet_cidr
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT n.name AS network_name, r.action AS rbac_action, MAX(n.mtu) AS max_mtu FROM NETWORKSECURITYBINDINGS AS nsb JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON nsb.network_id = n.id JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND r.action IN ('access_as_shared', 'access_as_external') GROUP BY n.name, r.action HAVING MAX(n.mtu) > 8000) SELECT i.network_name, i.rbac_action, i.max_mtu, COUNT(DISTINCT r2.target_tenant) AS distinct_target_tenants FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NETWORKS AS n2 ON n2.name = i.network_name JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r2 ON r2.object_id = n2.id AND r2.action = i.rbac_action GROUP BY i.network_name, i.rbac_action, i.max_mtu
WITH inner_cte AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.TENANT_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, b.VIF_TYPE, i.window_column, (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte) AS avg_window_column FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON i.PORT_ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE i.window_column > (SELECT AVG(window_column) FROM inner_cte)
