SELECT a.PORT_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, p.NETWORK_ID, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, ps.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY ps.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED ORDER BY p.NETWORK_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS a JOIN PORTS AS p ON a.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS ps ON p.ID = ps.PORT_ID WHERE a.IP_ADDRESS = '10.232.210.41/8' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')
SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))

SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_ipv4_dhcpopt_and_qos_policy FROM QOS_PORT_POLICY_BINDINGS AS qppb JOIN PORTS AS p ON qppb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN EXTRADHCPOPTS AS e ON p.ID = e.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND e.IP_VERSION = 4 AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')
SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.device_id IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7')
SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC
SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address
SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c'), Subq2 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), PortNetwork AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT pn.network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT s1.port_id) AS total_ports_in_security_group, COUNT(DISTINCT s2.port_id) AS total_down_ports, ROUND(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT s2.port_id) * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT s1.port_id), 0), 2) AS down_port_ratio FROM PortNetwork AS pn LEFT JOIN Subq1 AS s1 ON s1.port_id = pn.port_id LEFT JOIN Subq2 AS s2 ON s2.port_id = pn.port_id GROUP BY pn.network_id) SELECT network_id, total_ports_in_security_group, total_down_ports, down_port_ratio FROM inner_cte WHERE total_ports_in_security_group > 0 AND down_port_ratio > 0.50
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetWindowRanks AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SharedNetworkSubnetMaxIP AS (SELECT s.name, MAX(s.ip_version) AS max_ip_version, s.gateway_ip FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.object_id = s.network_id WHERE n.action = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY s.name, s.gateway_ip HAVING MAX(s.ip_version) > 3) SELECT swr.NAME AS subnet_name, swr.window_column, sns.gateway_ip, sns.max_ip_version, ROUND(CAST(sns.max_ip_version AS REAL) / NULLIF(swr.window_column, 0), 2) AS max_ip_version_per_window_rank FROM SubnetWindowRanks AS swr JOIN SharedNetworkSubnetMaxIP AS sns ON sns.name = swr.NAME ORDER BY max_ip_version_per_window_rank DESC) SELECT s.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT s.id) AS total_qualifying_subnets, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN inner_cte.max_ip_version_per_window_rank >= 2.00 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ratio_at_least_2 FROM inner_cte JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = inner_cte.subnet_name AND s.gateway_ip = inner_cte.gateway_ip GROUP BY s.tenant_id ORDER BY total_qualifying_subnets DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), Subq2 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.STATUS AS port_status, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = '0008e629-5619-41e0-b86c-4eecfeaacb6c') SELECT s1.port_id, s1.tenant_id, s1.mac_address, s2.port_status, s2.ip_address, s1.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, s2.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network FROM Subq1 AS s1 JOIN Subq2 AS s2 ON s1.port_id = s2.port_id ORDER BY s1.port_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.port_status, i.ip_address, i.running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, i.running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network, COUNT(p_all.id) AS total_ports_for_tenant, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_across_all_tenants FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p_all ON p_all.tenant_id = i.tenant_id, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(id) AS port_count FROM ports GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.port_status, i.ip_address, i.running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group, i.running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_network, avg_ports.avg_port_count ORDER BY i.port_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH down_ports_window AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), admin_up_ports_security AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_ports_with_admin_up_security_count FROM down_ports_window AS dpw JOIN admin_up_ports_security AS aups ON dpw.port_id = aups.port_id AND dpw.tenant_id = aups.tenant_id AND dpw.mac_address = aups.mac_address), TenantDownAdminUpCounts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS total_matching_ports FROM ports AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AverageTenantCount AS (SELECT AVG(total_matching_ports) AS avg_matching_ports FROM TenantDownAdminUpCounts) SELECT t.tenant_id, t.total_matching_ports, a.avg_matching_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN t.total_matching_ports > a.avg_matching_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN t.total_matching_ports < a.avg_matching_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category, inner_cte.down_ports_with_admin_up_security_count AS overall_matching_ports_count FROM TenantDownAdminUpCounts AS t CROSS JOIN AverageTenantCount AS a CROSS JOIN inner_cte
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), subq2 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), subq2_rollup AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT s2.port_id) AS admin_up_port_count, SUM(s2.binding_count) AS total_binding_count, SUM(s2.port_security_enabled_total) AS total_port_security_enabled FROM subq2 AS s2 JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = s2.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT s1.port_status, s1.device_owner, s1.port_count, s1.earliest_standard_attr_update, COALESCE(r.admin_up_port_count, 0) AS admin_up_port_count, COALESCE(r.total_binding_count, 0) AS total_binding_count, COALESCE(r.total_port_security_enabled, 0) AS total_port_security_enabled FROM subq1 AS s1 LEFT JOIN subq2_rollup AS r ON r.port_status = s1.port_status AND r.device_owner = s1.device_owner ORDER BY s1.port_count DESC) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, inner_cte.admin_up_port_count, inner_cte.total_binding_count, inner_cte.total_port_security_enabled FROM inner_cte WHERE inner_cte.port_count > (SELECT AVG(port_count) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY inner_cte.port_count DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortDownMacWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), PortDownIpRowNumber AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT p2.vif_type, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.port_id) AS Total_Down_Ports, AVG(p1.window_column) AS Avg_AdminStateUp_Window_Across_Ports, MAX(p2.window_column) AS Max_Ip_RowNumber_Window, COUNT(DISTINCT p2.tenant_id) AS Distinct_Tenants FROM PortDownIpRowNumber AS p2 JOIN PortDownMacWindow AS p1 ON p1.HOST = p2.host AND p1.VIF_TYPE = p2.vif_type GROUP BY p2.vif_type ORDER BY Total_Down_Ports DESC) SELECT vif_type, Total_Down_Ports, Avg_AdminStateUp_Window_Across_Ports, Max_Ip_RowNumber_Window, Distinct_Tenants FROM inner_cte WHERE Total_Down_Ports > (SELECT AVG(Total_Down_Ports) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY Total_Down_Ports DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortBindingsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), SecurityGroupPortRank AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPortSummary AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count, AVG(p.admin_state_up) AS avg_admin_state_up_down FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT sgpr.tenant_id, tds.down_port_count, ROUND(tds.avg_admin_state_up_down, 2) AS avg_admin_state_up_down, sgpr.security_group_id, sgpr.port_id, pbw.NAME AS port_name, sgpr.mac_address, pbw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, pbw.HOST AS binding_host, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_viftype_mac_window, sgpr.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, ROUND(CAST(pbw.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(tds.avg_admin_state_up_down, 0), 2) AS normalized_viftype_window_vs_tenant_avg FROM SecurityGroupPortRank AS sgpr JOIN PortBindingsWindow AS pbw ON pbw.MAC_ADDRESS = sgpr.mac_address JOIN TenantDownPortSummary AS tds ON tds.tenant_id = sgpr.tenant_id ORDER BY normalized_viftype_window_vs_tenant_avg DESC, sgpr.security_group_id, sgpr.port_id) SELECT inner_cte.*, tp.total_ports_all_statuses, ap.avg_port_count_across_tenants FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(id) AS total_ports_all_statuses FROM PORTS GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tp ON tp.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_across_tenants FROM (SELECT COUNT(id) AS port_count FROM PORTS GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS ap ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.normalized_viftype_window_vs_tenant_avg DESC, WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HapLoadBalancersByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1), ActiveBytesInByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT h.OPERATING_STATUS, h.total_loadbalancers, a.total_bytes_in FROM HapLoadBalancersByOpStatus AS h JOIN ActiveBytesInByOpStatus AS a ON h.OPERATING_STATUS = a.OPERATING_STATUS), ActiveHaproxyBytesByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS haproxy_active_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' AND lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS) SELECT i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.total_loadbalancers, i.total_bytes_in, ah.haproxy_active_bytes_in, (CAST(ah.haproxy_active_bytes_in * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(i.total_bytes_in, 0)) AS haproxy_active_bytes_in_pct FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ActiveHaproxyBytesByOpStatus AS ah ON i.OPERATING_STATUS = ah.OPERATING_STATUS

AVG(p.admin_state_up) AS avg_admin_state_up_down FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT sgpr.tenant_id, tds.down_port_count, ROUND(tds.avg_admin_state_up_down, 2) AS avg_admin_state_up_down, sgpr.security_group_id, sgpr.port_id, pbw.NAME AS port_name, sgpr.mac_address, pbw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, pbw.HOST AS binding_host, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_viftype_mac_window, sgpr.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, ROUND(CAST(pbw.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(tds.avg_admin_state_up_down, 0), 2) AS normalized_viftype_window_vs_tenant_avg FROM SecurityGroupPortRank AS sgpr JOIN PortBindingsWindow AS pbw ON pbw.MAC_ADDRESS = sgpr.mac_address JOIN TenantDownPortSummary AS tds ON tds.tenant_id = sgpr.tenant_id ORDER BY normalized_viftype_window_vs_tenant_avg DESC, sgpr.security_group_id, sgpr.port_id) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_down_ports, ROUND(AVG(i.avg_admin_state_up_down), 2) AS avg_admin_state_up_down, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN i.vif_type = 'ovs' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ovs_vif_type,
