WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT sgp.tenant_id, sgp.port_id, sgp.port_name, sgp.security_group_binding_count, sgp.dns_record_count, dpb.host, dpb.vif_type, dpb.ip_address, dpb.window_column FROM SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS sgp JOIN DownPortBindings AS dpb ON sgp.port_id = dpb.port_id WHERE sgp.security_group_binding_count >= 1 ORDER BY sgp.security_group_binding_count DESC, sgp.dns_record_count DESC, dpb.ip_address) SELECT i.tenant_id AS qualifying_ports_tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_qualifying_ports, avg_q.avg_qualifying_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(tenant_port_count) AS avg_qualifying_port_count FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS tenant_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS per_tenant) AS avg_q ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY i.tenant_id, avg_q.avg_qualifying_port_count ORDER BY total_qualifying_ports DESC, qualifying_ports_tenant_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), subq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT s2.PORT_ID AS port_id, s2.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, s2.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, s1.window_column AS down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type, s2.window_column AS drive_59_ports_running_sum_by_vif_type FROM subq2 AS s2 JOIN subq1 AS s1 ON s1.port_id = s2.PORT_ID AND s1.tenant_id = s2.TENANT_ID AND s1.mac_address = s2.MAC_ADDRESS ORDER BY s2.PORT_ID), benchmark_cte AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type) AS avg_down_running_sum FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type, i.drive_59_ports_running_sum_by_vif_type, b.avg_down_running_sum AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type > b.avg_down_running_sum * 1.5 THEN 'ABOVE_BENCHMARK' WHEN i.down_ports_running_sum_by_resource_type < b.avg_down_running_sum * 0.5 THEN 'BELOW_BENCHMARK' ELSE 'AROUND_BENCHMARK' END AS benchmark_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN benchmark_cte AS b ORDER BY i.port_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), ActiveNetworksWithAttrs AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), DownPortsOnActiveNetworks AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, dpb.NAME AS port_name, dpb.MAC_ADDRESS, dpb.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS port_admin_state_up, dpb.HOST AS binding_host, dpb.VIF_TYPE, dpb.window_column AS port_admin_state_up_avg_window_2_pre_2_fol, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant FROM PORTS AS p JOIN DownPortsWithBindings AS dpb ON p.MAC_ADDRESS = dpb.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.NAME = dpb.NAME AND p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = dpb.ADMIN_STATE_UP JOIN ActiveNetworksWithAttrs AS an ON an.network_id = p.NETWORK_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT * FROM DownPortsOnActiveNetworks ORDER BY tenant_id, network_id, MAC_ADDRESS), ActiveNetworkMtuAvg AS (SELECT AVG(n.MTU) AS avg_active_network_mtu FROM NETWORKS AS n WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT i.* FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN ActiveNetworkMtuAvg AS a WHERE i.mtu > a.avg_active_network_mtu ORDER BY i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.MAC_ADDRESS
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), PortSecurityBindingsSummary AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), CombinedPortDetails AS (SELECT sg.port_id, sg.tenant_id, sg.port_name, ps.mac_address, sg.security_group_binding_count, sg.dns_record_count, ps.binding_count AS port_security_binding_count, ps.port_security_enabled_total FROM SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS sg JOIN PortSecurityBindingsSummary AS ps ON sg.port_id = ps.port_id) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, port_name, mac_address, security_group_binding_count, dns_record_count, port_security_binding_count, port_security_enabled_total FROM CombinedPortDetails ORDER BY tenant_id, port_id), TenantQualifiedPorts AS (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte WHERE port_security_binding_count > 0 GROUP BY tenant_id), AverageQualifiedPorts AS (SELECT AVG(total_ports) AS avg_ports FROM TenantQualifiedPorts) SELECT tqp.tenant_id, tqp.total_ports, aqp.avg_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN tqp.total_ports > aqp.avg_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN tqp.total_ports < aqp.avg_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM TenantQualifiedPorts AS tqp CROSS JOIN AverageQualifiedPorts AS aqp
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityGroupAndDnsCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortBindingRanks AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpr.port_id, dpr.port_name, psc.tenant_id, dpr.mac_address, dpr.binding_host, dpr.vif_type, dpr.window_column AS down_status_rank, psc.security_group_binding_count, psc.dns_record_count FROM DownPortBindingRanks AS dpr JOIN PortSecurityGroupAndDnsCounts AS psc ON dpr.port_id = psc.port_id ORDER BY psc.security_group_binding_count DESC, psc.dns_record_count DESC, dpr.port_id) SELECT tenant_id, MAX(security_group_binding_count) AS max_security_group_bindings_on_a_down_port, MAX(dns_record_count) AS max_dns_records_on_a_down_port FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subnet_details AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), top_shared_networks AS (SELECT n.OBJECT_ID AS network_id, COUNT(*) AS subnet_count FROM NETWORKRBACS AS n JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared' GROUP BY n.OBJECT_ID ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 3) SELECT tsn.network_id, tsn.subnet_count, sd.NAME AS subnet_name, sd.TENANT_ID AS subnet_tenant_id, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP AS allocation_pool_first_ip, sd.LAST_IP AS allocation_pool_last_ip, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM top_shared_networks AS tsn JOIN subnet_details AS sd ON sd.NETWORK_ID = tsn.network_id ORDER BY tsn.subnet_count DESC, tsn.network_id, sd.NAME) SELECT i.network_id, i.subnet_count, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_tenant_id, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.allocation_pool_first_ip, i.allocation_pool_last_ip, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(max_standard_attr_id_next2) AS avg_max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM inner_cte) AS a ON 1 = 1 WHERE i.max_standard_attr_id_next2 > a.avg_max_standard_attr_id_next2 ORDER BY i.subnet_count DESC, i.network_id, i.subnet_name
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HapLoadBalancers AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.ID, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, lb.OPERATING_STATUS, AVG(lb.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY lb.NAME) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), OperatingStatusBytesIn AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT hlb.NAME, hlb.ID, hlb.VIP_ADDRESS, hlb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, hlb.OPERATING_STATUS, hlb.window_column, osbi.total_bytes_in FROM HapLoadBalancers AS hlb JOIN OperatingStatusBytesIn AS osbi ON hlb.OPERATING_STATUS = osbi.OPERATING_STATUS ORDER BY hlb.NAME), StatusPerLbConnections AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, lb.ID AS loadbalancer_id, SUM(s.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS per_lb_total_connections FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS, lb.ID), StatusMaxConnections AS (SELECT OPERATING_STATUS, MAX(per_lb_total_connections) AS max_per_lb_total_connections FROM StatusPerLbConnections GROUP BY OPERATING_STATUS) SELECT i.NAME, i.ID, i.VIP_ADDRESS, i.PROVISIONING_STATUS, i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.window_column, i.total_bytes_in, smc.max_per_lb_total_connections FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN StatusMaxConnections AS smc ON i.OPERATING_STATUS = smc.OPERATING_STATUS ORDER BY i.NAME
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortBindingsAndIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsWithSecurityGroups AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpbip.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dpbip.port_id) AS down_port_count, MIN(dpbip.ip_address) AS min_allocated_ip_address, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Bound_Security_Groups FROM DownPortBindingsAndIPs AS dpbip JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = dpbip.port_id JOIN DownPortsWithSecurityGroups AS dpsg ON dpsg.NETWORK_ID = p.NETWORK_ID AND dpsg.MAC_ADDRESS = p.MAC_ADDRESS AND dpsg.STATUS = p.STATUS WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY dpbip.tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT dpbip.port_id) >= 2 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC, min_allocated_ip_address ASC) SELECT tenant_id, down_port_count, min_allocated_ip_address, Has_Bound_Security_Groups FROM inner_cte WHERE down_port_count > (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY down_port_count DESC, min_allocated_ip_address ASC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ip_allocations_admin_up AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), down_ports_window AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT COUNT(*) AS admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_count, COUNT(DISTINCT dpw.port_id) AS down_ports_count FROM ip_allocations_admin_up AS iaau CROSS JOIN down_ports_window AS dpw), DownPortsPerSecurityGroup AS (SELECT sgpb.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dpw.port_id) AS down_ports_in_sg, MAX(inner_cte.admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_count) AS admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') AS dpw ON dpw.security_group_id = sgpb.security_group_id AND dpw.port_id = sgpb.port_id CROSS JOIN inner_cte GROUP BY sgpb.security_group_id) SELECT security_group_id, down_ports_in_sg, admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_count FROM DownPortsPerSecurityGroup WHERE down_ports_in_sg > (SELECT AVG(down_ports_in_sg) FROM DownPortsPerSecurityGroup)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT aup.TENANT_ID, aup.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT aup.PORT_ID) AS Admin_Up_Port_Count, COUNT(DISTINCT dpi.port_id) AS Down_Port_Count, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT dpi.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Down_Ports_With_IPs FROM AdminUpPorts AS aup LEFT JOIN DownPortsWithIPs AS dpi ON aup.TENANT_ID = dpi.tenant_id AND aup.VIF_TYPE = dpi.vif_type WHERE aup.window_column > 0 GROUP BY aup.TENANT_ID, aup.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY Admin_Up_Port_Count DESC, Down_Port_Count DESC) SELECT inner_cte.TENANT_ID, inner_cte.VIF_TYPE, inner_cte.Admin_Up_Port_Count, inner_cte.Down_Port_Count, inner_cte.Has_Down_Ports_With_IPs FROM inner_cte ORDER BY inner_cte.Admin_Up_Port_Count DESC, inner_cte.Down_Port_Count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH AllowedAddressPairsOnPort AS (SELECT aap.MAC_ADDRESS AS allowed_mac_address, aap.IP_ADDRESS AS allowed_ip_address, p.ID AS port_id, sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN ALLOWEDADDRESSPAIRS AS aap ON p.ID = aap.PORT_ID WHERE p.ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034' ORDER BY aap.IP_ADDRESS), AdminUpPortBindingDetails AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), AdminUpDetailsForTargetPort AS (SELECT * FROM AdminUpPortBindingDetails WHERE PORT_ID = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034') SELECT aap.allowed_mac_address, aap.allowed_ip_address, aap.port_id, aap.security_group_id, aap.window_column AS allowed_address_pairs_running_avg_standard_attr_id, t.HOST, t.VIF_TYPE, t.VNIC_TYPE, t.TENANT_ID, t.NETWORK_ID, t.MAC_ADDRESS, t.STATUS, t.DEVICE_OWNER, t.window_column AS admin_up_ports_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM AllowedAddressPairsOnPort AS aap LEFT JOIN AdminUpDetailsForTargetPort AS t ON aap.port_id = t.PORT_ID ORDER BY aap.allowed_ip_address), TargetPortNetwork AS (SELECT p.network_id FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.id = '25d421c1-c76d-4c26-9550-9f871a1d5034'), NetworkSecurityGroups AS (SELECT p.network_id, p.id AS port_id, COUNT(DISTINCT spb.security_group_id) AS security_group_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.id = spb.port_id JOIN TargetPortNetwork AS tpn ON p.network_id = tpn.network_id GROUP BY p.network_id, p.id), NetworkMaxSecurityGroups AS (SELECT network_id, MAX(security_group_count) AS max_security_groups_on_any_port FROM NetworkSecurityGroups GROUP BY network_id) SELECT i.allowed_mac_address, i.allowed_ip_address, i.port_id, i.security_group_id, i.allowed_address_pairs_running_avg_standard_attr_id, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.admin_up_ports_window_avg_standard_attr_id, nmsg.max_security_groups_on_any_port FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkMaxSecurityGroups AS nmsg ON i.NETWORK_ID = nmsg.network_id ORDER BY i.allowed_ip_address
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), subq2 AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT subq1.port_id, subq1.tenant_id, subq2.name AS port_name, subq2.network_id, subq1.mac_address, subq2.admin_state_up, subq2.status, subq2.device_owner, subq1.security_group_id, subq1.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, subq2.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_in_3_preceding_1_following_by_port_id FROM subq1 JOIN subq2 ON subq1.security_group_id = subq2.security_group_id AND subq1.mac_address = subq2.mac_address ORDER BY subq1.security_group_id, subq1.window_column, subq1.port_id) SELECT inner_cte.port_id, inner_cte.tenant_id, inner_cte.port_name, inner_cte.network_id, inner_cte.mac_address, inner_cte.admin_state_up, inner_cte.status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.security_group_id, inner_cte.port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, inner_cte.min_admin_state_up_in_3_preceding_1_following_by_port_id, tenant_stats.total_down_ports, avg_stats.avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports FROM PORTS WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_stats ON tenant_stats.tenant_id = inner_cte.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports_across_tenants FROM (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM PORTS WHERE status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY tenant_id) AS per_tenant) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.security_group_id, inner_cte.port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, inner_cte.port_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.ID, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, lb.OPERATING_STATUS, AVG(lb.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY lb.NAME) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), subq2 AS (SELECT lb.ID AS loadbalancer_id, lb.NAME AS loadbalancer_name, pra.PROVIDER_NAME AS provider_name, lb.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = lb.ID JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE lb.OPERATING_STATUS = 'ONLINE') SELECT s1.NAME AS load_balancer_name, s1.ID AS load_balancer_id, s1.VIP_ADDRESS AS vip_address, s1.PROVISIONING_STATUS AS provisioning_status, s1.OPERATING_STATUS AS operating_status, s1.window_column AS running_avg_loadbalancer_id_within_provider_ordered_by_name, s2.provider_name, s2.window_column AS rank_within_provider_by_total_connections FROM subq1 AS s1 JOIN subq2 AS s2 ON s1.ID = s2.loadbalancer_id ORDER BY s1.NAME), AvgConnections AS (SELECT AVG(stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS) AS avg_total_connections FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = i.load_balancer_id) SELECT i.load_balancer_name, i.load_balancer_id, i.vip_address, i.provisioning_status, i.operating_status, i.running_avg_loadbalancer_id_within_provider_ordered_by_name, i.provider_name, i.rank_within_provider_by_total_connections, stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS AS total_connections, (stats.TOTAL_CONNECTIONS - ac.avg_total_connections) AS deviation_from_avg_total_connections FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS stats ON stats.LOADBALANCER_ID = i.load_balancer_id CROSS JOIN AvgConnections AS ac ORDER BY i.load_balancer_name
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), Drive59PortsRunningSum AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), DownDrive59PortDetails AS (SELECT dpw.NAME, dpw.MAC_ADDRESS, dpw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpw.HOST, dpw.VIF_TYPE, dpw.window_column AS down_ports_avg_admin_state_up_window, d59.PORT_ID, d59.TENANT_ID, d59.window_column AS drive59_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM DownPortsWindow AS dpw JOIN Drive59PortsRunningSum AS d59 ON dpw.MAC_ADDRESS = d59.MAC_ADDRESS WHERE dpw.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT * FROM DownDrive59PortDetails ORDER BY VIF_TYPE, MAC_ADDRESS, PORT_ID), avg_ports AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(p2.ID) AS port_count FROM PORTS AS p2 GROUP BY p2.TENANT_ID) AS sub), tenant_port_counts AS (SELECT p3.TENANT_ID, COUNT(p3.ID) AS total_ports FROM PORTS AS p3 GROUP BY p3.TENANT_ID) SELECT i.*, tpc.total_ports, ap.avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN tenant_port_counts AS tpc ON i.TENANT_ID = tpc.TENANT_ID, avg_ports AS ap ORDER BY i.VIF_TYPE, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.PORT_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), q2 AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT q2.network_id, q2.network_name, q2.tenant_id, q2.admin_state_up, q2.mtu, q2.standard_attr_updated_at, q1.port_id, q1.mac_address, q1.security_group_id, q1.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, q2.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant FROM q2 JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = q2.network_id JOIN q1 ON q1.port_id = p.id AND q1.tenant_id = q2.tenant_id ORDER BY q2.tenant_id, q2.network_name, q1.security_group_id, q1.window_column, q1.port_id), avg_active_mtu AS (SELECT AVG(n.mtu) AS avg_mtu FROM NETWORKS AS n WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE') SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.port_id, i.mac_address, i.security_group_id, i.port_rank_within_security_group, i.network_row_number_within_tenant, (i.mtu - a.avg_mtu) AS deviation_from_avg_active_network_mtu FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_active_mtu AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH default_external_network_rbac_rank AS (SELECT n.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY r.ACTION ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS r ON n.ID = r.OBJECT_ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0), default_external_network_status_admin_avg AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, AVG(n.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY n.STATUS ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0) SELECT s.NETWORK_ID, s.NAME, s.STATUS, s.ADMIN_STATE_UP, s.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_within_status_by_name, r.window_column AS dense_rank_by_rbac_action_with_2_preceding_frame FROM default_external_network_status_admin_avg AS s JOIN default_external_network_rbac_rank AS r ON s.NAME = r.NAME) SELECT i.NETWORK_ID, i.NAME, i.STATUS, i.ADMIN_STATE_UP, i.avg_admin_state_up_within_status_by_name, i.dense_rank_by_rbac_action_with_2_preceding_frame, overall_avg.overall_avg_of_status_group_avgs FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(avg_admin_state_up_within_status_by_name) AS overall_avg_of_status_group_avgs FROM inner_cte) AS overall_avg ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subnet_shared_rbac AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), active_network_rbac AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE') SELECT anr.name AS network_name, anr.status AS network_status, anr.admin_state_up, anr.action AS rbac_action, anr.target_tenant AS rbac_target_tenant, anr.window_column AS network_dense_rank, ssr.subnet_name, ssr.subnet_cidr, ssr.window_column AS subnet_standard_attr_running_sum FROM active_network_rbac AS anr JOIN subnet_shared_rbac AS ssr ON ssr.rbac_target_tenant = anr.target_tenant AND anr.action = 'access_as_shared'), avg_running_sum AS (SELECT AVG(subnet_standard_attr_running_sum) AS avg_subnet_running_sum FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_name, i.network_status, i.admin_state_up, i.rbac_action, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.network_dense_rank, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.subnet_standard_attr_running_sum, (i.subnet_standard_attr_running_sum - a.avg_subnet_running_sum) AS deviation_from_avg_running_sum FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_running_sum AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_status_owner_stats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), bound_admin_ports AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), top10_status_owner_by_bound_ports AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS bound_admin_port_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY bound_admin_port_count DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT t.port_status, t.device_owner, t.bound_admin_port_count, psos.port_count AS total_ports_with_standard_attr_resource_type_ports, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update, SUM(bap.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings_across_bound_admin_ports, SUM(bap.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records_across_bound_admin_ports FROM top10_status_owner_by_bound_ports AS t JOIN port_status_owner_stats AS psos ON psos.port_status = t.port_status AND ((psos.device_owner = t.device_owner) OR (psos.device_owner IS NULL AND t.device_owner IS NULL)) JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.STATUS = t.port_status AND ((p.DEVICE_OWNER = t.device_owner) OR (p.DEVICE_OWNER IS NULL AND t.device_owner IS NULL)) JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AND sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' JOIN bound_admin_ports AS bap ON bap.port_id = p.id GROUP BY t.port_status, t.device_owner, t.bound_admin_port_count, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY t.bound_admin_port_count DESC) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.bound_admin_port_count, i.total_ports_with_standard_attr_resource_type_ports, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.total_security_group_bindings_across_bound_admin_ports, i.total_dns_records_across_bound_admin_ports, avg_stats.avg_bound_admin_port_count_across_top10 FROM inner_cte AS i, (SELECT AVG(bound_admin_port_count) AS avg_bound_admin_port_count_across_top10 FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HapLoadBalancers AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.ID, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, lb.OPERATING_STATUS, AVG(lb.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY lb.NAME) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), OperatingStatusBytesIn AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT hlb.NAME, hlb.ID, hlb.VIP_ADDRESS, hlb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, hlb.OPERATING_STATUS, hlb.window_column, osbi.total_bytes_in FROM HapLoadBalancers AS hlb JOIN OperatingStatusBytesIn AS osbi ON hlb.OPERATING_STATUS = osbi.OPERATING_STATUS WHERE hlb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' ORDER BY hlb.NAME), AvgBytesInBenchmark AS (SELECT AVG(total_bytes_in) AS avg_total_bytes_in FROM (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) AS t) SELECT i.NAME, i.ID, i.VIP_ADDRESS, i.PROVISIONING_STATUS, i.OPERATING_STATUS, i.window_column, i.total_bytes_in, a.avg_total_bytes_in AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.total_bytes_in > a.avg_total_bytes_in * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_BYTES_IN' WHEN i.total_bytes_in < a.avg_total_bytes_in * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_BYTES_IN' ELSE 'AVERAGE_BYTES_IN' END AS bytes_in_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgBytesInBenchmark AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), PortIpBindingWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT pibw.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT pibw.port_id) AS down_port_count, AVG(pibw.window_column) AS avg_ip_row_number_window, AVG(psw.window_column) AS avg_port_security_dense_rank_window FROM PortIpBindingWindow AS pibw JOIN PortSecurityWindow AS psw ON pibw.port_id = psw.port_id WHERE NOT pibw.tenant_id IS NULL GROUP BY pibw.tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT pibw.port_id) >= 2 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC, pibw.tenant_id), Benchmark AS (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.down_port_count, i.avg_ip_row_number_window, i.avg_port_security_dense_rank_window, b.avg_down_ports AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.down_port_count > b.avg_down_ports * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_DOWN' WHEN i.down_port_count < b.avg_down_ports * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_DOWN' ELSE 'AVERAGE_DOWN' END AS down_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN Benchmark AS b
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dp.NAME AS down_port_name, dp.MAC_ADDRESS AS down_mac_address, dp.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS down_admin_state_up, dp.HOST AS binding_host, dp.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, dp.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f, au.port_name AS admin_up_port_name, au.mac_address AS admin_up_mac_address, au.port_security_enabled, au.window_column AS row_number_window_2p_1f FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dp JOIN AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS au ON dp.MAC_ADDRESS = au.mac_address ORDER BY dp.MAC_ADDRESS), overall_avg AS (SELECT AVG(avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f) AS overall_avg_admin_state_up_window FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.down_port_name, i.down_mac_address, i.down_admin_state_up, i.binding_host, i.vif_type, i.avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f, i.admin_up_port_name, i.admin_up_mac_address, i.port_security_enabled, i.row_number_window_2p_1f, (i.avg_admin_state_up_window_2p_2f - oa.overall_avg_admin_state_up_window) AS deviation_from_overall_avg_windowed_admin_state_up FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN overall_avg AS oa ORDER BY i.down_mac_address
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.network_id, an.network_name, an.tenant_id, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, an.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, ap.PORT_ID, ap.HOST, ap.VIF_TYPE, ap.VNIC_TYPE, ap.MAC_ADDRESS, ap.STATUS AS port_status, ap.DEVICE_OWNER, ap.window_column AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_2_preceding_2_following FROM ActiveNetworks AS an JOIN AdminUpPorts AS ap ON ap.NETWORK_ID = an.network_id AND ap.TENANT_ID = an.tenant_id), TenantActiveAdminUpPortCounts AS (SELECT n.tenant_id, n.id AS network_id, COUNT(p.id) AS admin_up_port_count FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.network_id = n.id AND p.tenant_id = n.tenant_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY n.tenant_id, n.id), TenantActiveAdminUpAverages AS (SELECT tenant_id, AVG(admin_up_port_count) AS avg_admin_up_ports_per_active_network FROM TenantActiveAdminUpPortCounts GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT i.network_id, i.network_name, i.tenant_id, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.network_row_number_within_tenant, i.port_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.vnic_type, i.mac_address, i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.avg_standard_attr_id_window_2_preceding_2_following FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantActiveAdminUpPortCounts AS c ON c.tenant_id = i.tenant_id AND c.network_id = i.network_id JOIN TenantActiveAdminUpAverages AS a ON a.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE c.admin_up_port_count > a.avg_admin_up_ports_per_active_network
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HostDrive59Ports AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), Drive59AdminUpSummary AS (SELECT h.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT h.PORT_ID) AS drive59_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.port_security_enabled = 1 THEN h.PORT_ID END) AS drive59_secured_port_count, ROUND(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.port_security_enabled = 1 THEN h.PORT_ID END) * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT h.PORT_ID), 0), 2) AS secured_port_ratio FROM HostDrive59Ports AS h JOIN AdminUpPorts AS a ON a.mac_address = h.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY h.TENANT_ID) SELECT s.TENANT_ID, s.drive59_port_count, s.drive59_secured_port_count, s.secured_port_ratio FROM Drive59AdminUpSummary AS s ORDER BY s.secured_port_ratio DESC, s.drive59_port_count DESC) SELECT inner_cte.TENANT_ID, inner_cte.drive59_port_count, inner_cte.drive59_secured_port_count, inner_cte.secured_port_ratio, avg_stats.avg_secured_port_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(secured_port_ratio) AS avg_secured_port_ratio FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY inner_cte.secured_port_ratio DESC, inner_cte.drive59_port_count DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE') SELECT an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at, COUNT(DISTINCT dp.port_id) AS down_port_count FROM ActiveNetworks AS an LEFT JOIN DownPorts AS dp ON dp.network_id = an.network_id AND dp.tenant_id = an.tenant_id WHERE an.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY an.tenant_id, an.network_id, an.network_name, an.admin_state_up, an.mtu, an.standard_attr_updated_at HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT dp.port_id) > 0 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC, an.network_name), DownPortAverages AS (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.network_name, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.down_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i WHERE i.down_port_count > (SELECT avg_down_ports FROM DownPortAverages) ORDER BY i.down_port_count DESC, i.network_name
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH HostDrive59Ports AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), Drive59AdminUpSummary AS (SELECT h.TENANT_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT h.PORT_ID) AS drive59_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.port_security_enabled = 1 THEN h.PORT_ID END) AS drive59_secured_port_count, ROUND(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN a.port_security_enabled = 1 THEN h.PORT_ID END) * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT h.PORT_ID), 0), 2) AS secured_port_ratio FROM HostDrive59Ports AS h JOIN AdminUpPorts AS a ON a.mac_address = h.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY h.TENANT_ID) SELECT s.TENANT_ID, s.drive59_port_count, s.drive59_secured_port_count, s.secured_port_ratio FROM Drive59AdminUpSummary AS s ORDER BY s.secured_port_ratio DESC, s.drive59_port_count DESC), avg_cte AS (SELECT AVG(secured_port_ratio) AS avg_secured_port_ratio FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.TENANT_ID, i.drive59_port_count, i.drive59_secured_port_count, i.secured_port_ratio, (i.secured_port_ratio - a.avg_secured_port_ratio) AS deviation_from_avg_secured_port_ratio FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_cte AS a ORDER BY deviation_from_avg_secured_port_ratio DESC, i.secured_port_ratio DESC, i.drive59_port_count DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortBindingIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortMacAttrSums AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d1.port_id, d1.tenant_id, d1.host, d1.vif_type, d1.ip_address, d1.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, d2.mac_address, d2.window_column AS standard_attr_running_sum_window FROM DownPortBindingIPs AS d1 JOIN DownPortMacAttrSums AS d2 ON d1.port_id = d2.port_id ORDER BY d1.tenant_id, d1.port_id, d1.ip_address), TenantDownPortCounts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS down_ports FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id), AverageTenantDownPortCounts AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports) AS avg_down_ports FROM TenantDownPortCounts) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.ip_row_number_window, i.mac_address, i.standard_attr_running_sum_window, t.down_ports AS tenant_down_port_count, (t.down_ports - a.avg_down_ports) AS deviation_from_avg_tenant_down_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantDownPortCounts AS t ON t.tenant_id = i.tenant_id CROSS JOIN AverageTenantDownPortCounts AS a ORDER BY i.tenant_id, i.port_id, i.ip_address
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), PortIpBindingWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT pibw.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT pibw.port_id) AS down_port_count, AVG(pibw.window_column) AS avg_ip_row_number_window, AVG(psw.window_column) AS avg_port_security_dense_rank_window FROM PortIpBindingWindow AS pibw JOIN PortSecurityWindow AS psw ON pibw.port_id = psw.port_id WHERE NOT pibw.tenant_id IS NULL GROUP BY pibw.tenant_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT pibw.port_id) >= 2 ORDER BY down_port_count DESC, pibw.tenant_id) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.down_port_count, i.avg_ip_row_number_window, i.avg_port_security_dense_rank_window, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT b.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_port_binding FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN ports AS p ON p.tenant_id = i.tenant_id LEFT JOIN ml2_port_bindings AS b ON b.port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.tenant_id, i.down_port_count, i.avg_ip_row_number_window, i.avg_port_security_dense_rank_window ORDER BY i.down_port_count DESC, i.tenant_id LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsStdAttrWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsBindingRank AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT s.port_id, s.tenant_id, s.mac_address, s.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_ids_within_resource_type, b.port_name, b.binding_host, b.vif_type, b.window_column AS rank_within_down_status_by_port_id FROM DownPortsStdAttrWindow AS s JOIN DownPortsBindingRank AS b ON s.port_id = b.port_id AND s.mac_address = b.mac_address) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, running_sum_standard_attr_ids_within_resource_type, port_name, binding_host, vif_type, rank_within_down_status_by_port_id FROM inner_cte WHERE rank_within_down_status_by_port_id > (SELECT AVG(rank_within_down_status_by_port_id) FROM inner_cte)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ia.SUBNET_ID AS subnet_id, AVG(p.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS), CombinedPorts AS (SELECT a.PORT_ID AS port_id, a.HOST, a.VIF_TYPE, a.VNIC_TYPE, a.TENANT_ID, a.NETWORK_ID, a.MAC_ADDRESS, a.STATUS, a.DEVICE_OWNER, a.window_column AS admin_up_window_avg_standard_attr_id, NULL AS port_name, NULL AS ip_address, NULL AS subnet_id, NULL AS down_window_avg_port_id, 'Admin State Up' AS reason FROM AdminUpPorts AS a UNION ALL SELECT d.port_id, NULL AS HOST, NULL AS VIF_TYPE, NULL AS VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, d.mac_address AS MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, NULL AS admin_up_window_avg_standard_attr_id, d.port_name, d.ip_address, d.subnet_id, d.window_column AS down_window_avg_port_id, 'Status DOWN' AS reason FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = d.port_id WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT * FROM CombinedPorts ORDER BY port_id, reason), NetworkPortWindowMetric AS (SELECT p.network_id, p.id AS port_id, MAX(COALESCE(inner_cte.admin_up_window_avg_standard_attr_id, inner_cte.down_window_avg_port_id)) AS port_window_metric FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.id = inner_cte.port_id GROUP BY p.network_id, p.id) SELECT network_id, MAX(port_window_metric) AS max_port_window_metric FROM NetworkPortWindowMetric GROUP BY network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_status_owner_sg_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), down_ports_with_ip_window AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.port_status, s.device_owner, s.port_count, s.min_standard_attr_id FROM down_ports_with_ip_window AS d JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = d.port_id JOIN port_status_owner_sg_summary AS s ON s.port_status = p.STATUS AND s.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, MAX(i.window_column) AS max_ip_row_number FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = i.port_id JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON spb.PORT_ID = p.ID GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), ActiveTenantPortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = atn.network_id JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos ON psos.port_status = p.STATUS AND psos.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa_p ON sa_p.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa_p.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update) SELECT * FROM ActiveTenantPortStatusOwnerStats ORDER BY tenant_id, window_column, network_id, port_status, device_owner), StatusAverages AS (SELECT port_status, AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID JOIN (SELECT p2.STATUS AS port_status, p2.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p2.ID) AS port_count FROM PORTS AS p2 JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p2.STATUS, p2.DEVICE_OWNER) AS psos ON psos.port_status = p.STATUS AND psos.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count) AS grouped_counts GROUP BY port_status) SELECT i.* FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN StatusAverages AS sa ON sa.port_status = i.port_status WHERE i.port_count > sa.avg_port_count ORDER BY i.tenant_id, i.window_column, i.network_id, i.port_status, i.device_owner
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortSecurityStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT d.port_id, d.port_name, d.mac_address, d.binding_host, d.vif_type, d.window_column, a.tenant_id, a.binding_count, a.port_security_enabled_total FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS d JOIN AdminUpPortSecurityStats AS a ON d.port_id = a.port_id WHERE a.port_security_enabled_total > 0 ORDER BY a.port_security_enabled_total DESC, d.port_id), HostPortSecurity AS (SELECT binding_host, port_id, MAX(port_security_enabled_total) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM inner_cte GROUP BY binding_host, port_id) SELECT binding_host, MAX(port_security_enabled_total) AS max_port_security_enabled_total FROM HostPortSecurity GROUP BY binding_host
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortBindingsAndIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsMacAndStdAttrRunningSum AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d1.port_id, d1.tenant_id, d1.host, d1.vif_type, d1.ip_address, d1.window_column AS ip_row_number_window, d2.mac_address, d2.window_column AS stdattr_id_running_sum_window FROM DownPortBindingsAndIPs AS d1 JOIN DownPortsMacAndStdAttrRunningSum AS d2 ON d1.port_id = d2.port_id AND d1.tenant_id = d2.tenant_id), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, AVG(sa.ID) AS avg_stdattr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY b.VIF_TYPE) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.ip_row_number_window, i.mac_address, i.stdattr_id_running_sum_window FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = i.port_id JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.ID > (SELECT v.avg_stdattr_id FROM VifTypeAverages AS v WHERE v.vif_type = i.vif_type)
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SharedSubnets AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), ActiveNetworkRBACs AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE') SELECT anr.name AS network_name, anr.status AS network_status, anr.admin_state_up, anr.action AS rbac_action, anr.target_tenant AS rbac_target_tenant, ss.subnet_name, ss.subnet_cidr, ss.window_column AS subnet_std_attr_running_sum_by_ip_version, anr.window_column AS network_dense_rank_within_network FROM ActiveNetworkRBACs AS anr JOIN SharedSubnets AS ss ON ss.rbac_target_tenant = anr.target_tenant AND anr.action = 'access_as_shared' ORDER BY anr.name, ss.subnet_name) SELECT i.network_name, i.network_status, i.admin_state_up, i.rbac_action, i.rbac_target_tenant, i.subnet_name, i.subnet_cidr, i.subnet_std_attr_running_sum_by_ip_version, i.network_dense_rank_within_network, n.standard_attr_id AS network_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN networks AS n ON n.name = i.network_name WHERE i.subnet_std_attr_running_sum_by_ip_version > 50000 ORDER BY i.network_name, i.subnet_name
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DefaultExternalNetworks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, AVG(n.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY n.STATUS ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0), CombinedResults AS (SELECT den.NETWORK_ID AS entity_id, den.NAME AS entity_name, den.STATUS AS status, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, den.window_column AS metric_value, 'Default External Network' AS record_type FROM DefaultExternalNetworks AS den UNION ALL SELECT NULL AS entity_id, NULL AS entity_name, psos.port_status AS status, NULL AS admin_state_up, psos.port_count AS metric_value, 'Port Count by Status/Owner (ports resource_type)' AS record_type FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos) SELECT record_type, entity_id, entity_name, status, admin_state_up, metric_value FROM CombinedResults ORDER BY record_type, status, entity_name, entity_id), avg_metrics AS (SELECT AVG(metric_value) AS avg_metric_value FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.record_type, i.entity_id, i.entity_name, i.status, i.admin_state_up, i.metric_value, (i.metric_value - a.avg_metric_value) AS deviation_from_overall_avg_metric_value FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_metrics AS a ORDER BY i.record_type, i.status, i.entity_name, i.entity_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortIpAllocSummary AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), PortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), CombinedPortDetails AS (SELECT s.port_id, s.tenant_id, s.mac_address, w.port_name, w.port_security_enabled, w.window_column, s.ip_allocation_count, s.subnet_id_rows FROM PortIpAllocSummary AS s JOIN PortSecurityWindow AS w ON w.mac_address = s.mac_address) SELECT * FROM CombinedPortDetails ORDER BY tenant_id, mac_address, port_id), AvgIpAlloc AS (SELECT AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.port_name, i.port_security_enabled, i.window_column, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows, a.avg_ip_allocation_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.ip_allocation_count > a.avg_ip_allocation_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_IP_ALLOC' WHEN i.ip_allocation_count < a.avg_ip_allocation_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_IP_ALLOC' ELSE 'AVERAGE_IP_ALLOC' END AS ip_alloc_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgIpAlloc AS a ORDER BY i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.port_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), Subq2 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT q2.NAME AS subnet_name, q2.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, q2.TENANT_ID AS subnet_tenant_id, q2.NETWORK_ID AS subnet_network_id, q2.IP_VERSION AS ip_version, q2.GATEWAY_IP AS gateway_ip, q2.ENABLE_DHCP AS enable_dhcp, q2.FIRST_IP AS allocation_pool_first_ip, q2.LAST_IP AS allocation_pool_last_ip, q1.rbac_target_tenant, q1.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_ip_version, q2.max_standard_attr_id_next2, ROUND(CAST(q2.max_standard_attr_id_next2 AS REAL) / NULLIF(q1.window_column, 0), 2) AS max_to_running_sum_ratio FROM Subq2 AS q2 JOIN Subq1 AS q1 ON q1.subnet_name = q2.NAME AND q1.subnet_cidr = q2.CIDR ORDER BY max_to_running_sum_ratio DESC) SELECT subnet_name, subnet_cidr, subnet_tenant_id, subnet_network_id, ip_version, gateway_ip, enable_dhcp, allocation_pool_first_ip, allocation_pool_last_ip, rbac_target_tenant, running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_ip_version, max_standard_attr_id_next2, max_to_running_sum_ratio FROM inner_cte WHERE max_to_running_sum_ratio > (SELECT AVG(max_to_running_sum_ratio) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY max_to_running_sum_ratio DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortStandardAttrWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortBindingRank AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpsaw.port_id, dpsaw.tenant_id, dpbr.port_name, dpsaw.mac_address, dpbr.binding_host, dpbr.vif_type, dpsaw.window_column AS standard_attr_id_running_sum_within_resource_type, dpbr.window_column AS port_rank_within_down_status FROM DownPortStandardAttrWindow AS dpsaw JOIN DownPortBindingRank AS dpbr ON dpsaw.port_id = dpbr.port_id AND dpsaw.mac_address = dpbr.mac_address ORDER BY dpsaw.port_id) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_down_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN i.vif_type = 'ovs' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ovs_vif_type_on_any_down_port FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_down_ports DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS) SELECT psa.port_status, psa.device_owner, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id, dpw.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_vif_type, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_window_admin_state_up FROM PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psa CROSS JOIN DownPortsWindow AS dpw GROUP BY psa.port_status, psa.device_owner, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id, dpw.VIF_TYPE) SELECT VIF_TYPE, MAX(distinct_device_owners) AS max_distinct_device_owners FROM (SELECT VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT device_owner) AS distinct_device_owners FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE) AS t GROUP BY VIF_TYPE
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_status_owner_sg_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), down_ports_with_ip_window AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, b.HOST AS host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.host, d.vif_type, d.ip_address, d.window_column, s.port_status, s.device_owner, s.port_count, s.min_standard_attr_id FROM down_ports_with_ip_window AS d JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = d.port_id JOIN port_status_owner_sg_summary AS s ON s.port_status = p.STATUS AND s.device_owner = p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.window_column, i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, avg_counts.avg_port_count_across_down_status_owner_groups FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_across_down_status_owner_groups FROM (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) AS sub) AS avg_counts ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH down_ports_bindings AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), down_ports_ipallocs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ia.SUBNET_ID AS subnet_id, AVG(p.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS) SELECT dpi.port_id, dpi.port_name, dpi.mac_address, dpb.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpb.HOST, dpb.VIF_TYPE, dpi.ip_address, dpi.subnet_id, dpb.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_window_2_pre_2_fol_by_vif_type, dpi.window_column AS avg_port_id_by_port_id_ordered_by_ip FROM down_ports_ipallocs AS dpi JOIN down_ports_bindings AS dpb ON dpi.port_name = dpb.NAME AND dpi.mac_address = dpb.MAC_ADDRESS ORDER BY dpi.ip_address) SELECT i.port_id, i.port_name, i.mac_address, i.admin_state_up, i.host, i.vif_type, i.ip_address, i.subnet_id, i.avg_admin_state_up_window_2_pre_2_fol_by_vif_type, i.avg_port_id_by_port_id_ordered_by_ip, vif_stats.total_down_ports_for_vif_type, avg_vif_stats.avg_down_ports_across_vif_types FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT vif_type, COUNT(*) AS total_down_ports_for_vif_type FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vif_type) AS vif_stats ON i.vif_type = vif_stats.vif_type JOIN (SELECT AVG(down_port_count) AS avg_down_ports_across_vif_types FROM (SELECT COUNT(*) AS down_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vif_type) AS sub) AS avg_vif_stats ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY i.ip_address
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortIpWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ia.SUBNET_ID AS subnet_id, AVG(p.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS), DownPortNetworkRowNum AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpiw.port_id, dpnr.tenant_id, dpiw.port_name, dpnr.network_id, dpiw.mac_address, dpnr.device_owner, dpiw.ip_address, dpiw.subnet_id, dpiw.window_column AS avg_port_id_within_port_id_group_by_ip, dpnr.window_column AS row_number_within_network_by_port_id FROM DownPortIpWindow AS dpiw JOIN DownPortNetworkRowNum AS dpnr ON dpiw.port_id = dpnr.port_id AND dpiw.ip_address = dpnr.ip_address ORDER BY dpnr.network_id, dpnr.window_column, dpiw.ip_address), NetworkDownPortCounts AS (SELECT p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS total_down_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.NETWORK_ID) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.device_owner, i.ip_address, i.subnet_id, i.avg_port_id_within_port_id_group_by_ip, i.row_number_within_network_by_port_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN NetworkDownPortCounts AS ndpc ON i.network_id = ndpc.network_id WHERE ndpc.total_down_ports > (SELECT AVG(total_down_ports) FROM NetworkDownPortCounts) ORDER BY i.network_id, i.row_number_within_network_by_port_id, i.ip_address
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), Drive59PortsRunningSum AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), DownDrive59PortDetails AS (SELECT dpw.NAME, dpw.MAC_ADDRESS, dpw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpw.HOST, dpw.VIF_TYPE, dpw.window_column AS down_ports_avg_admin_state_up_window, d59.PORT_ID, d59.TENANT_ID, d59.window_column AS drive59_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM DownPortsWindow AS dpw JOIN Drive59PortsRunningSum AS d59 ON dpw.MAC_ADDRESS = d59.MAC_ADDRESS WHERE dpw.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT * FROM DownDrive59PortDetails ORDER BY VIF_TYPE, MAC_ADDRESS, PORT_ID) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT psb.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_port_security_binding FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.id = i.port_id LEFT JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ports DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), Drive59PortsByVifType AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), CombinedPorts AS (SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column, 'DOWN (by Security Group)' AS reason FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup UNION ALL SELECT PORT_ID AS port_id, TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, window_column, 'Host drive-59 (by VIF Type)' AS reason FROM Drive59PortsByVifType) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, window_column, reason FROM CombinedPorts ORDER BY tenant_id, port_id) SELECT inner_cte.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.port_id) AS total_ports_in_combined_set, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN inner_cte.reason = 'Host drive-59 (by VIF Type)' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_drive59_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ports_in_combined_set DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dpwb.port_id, dpwb.port_name, dpwb.mac_address, dpwb.binding_host, dpwb.vif_type, dpwb.window_column AS down_status_rank, aupws.port_security_enabled, aupws.window_column AS admin_up_row_number FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dpwb JOIN AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS aupws ON dpwb.mac_address = aupws.mac_address WHERE aupws.port_security_enabled = 1 ORDER BY dpwb.port_id) SELECT binding_host, MAX(down_status_rank) AS max_down_status_rank FROM inner_cte GROUP BY binding_host
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsWithRowNumbers AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dpsg.port_id, dpsg.tenant_id, dpsg.mac_address, dpsg.window_column AS down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id, aup.port_name, aup.port_security_enabled, aup.window_column AS admin_up_ports_row_number FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg JOIN AdminUpPortsWithRowNumbers AS aup ON dpsg.mac_address = aup.mac_address WHERE dpsg.window_column > 0 ORDER BY dpsg.window_column DESC, dpsg.mac_address), AverageRunningSum AS (SELECT AVG(down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id) AS avg_running_sum FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.mac_address, i.down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id, i.port_name, i.port_security_enabled, i.admin_up_ports_row_number, (i.down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id - a.avg_running_sum) AS deviation_from_avg_running_sum FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AverageRunningSum AS a ORDER BY i.down_ports_running_sum_standard_attr_id DESC, i.mac_address
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS), CombinedPortStatusMetrics AS (SELECT sg.port_status AS status, sg.device_owner AS device_owner, sg.port_count AS port_count, sg.min_standard_attr_id AS min_standard_attr_id, NULL AS min_admin_state_up, NULL AS avg_standard_attr_id, 'Security Group Bound Ports' AS reason FROM SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS sg UNION ALL SELECT ov.STATUS AS status, NULL AS device_owner, NULL AS port_count, NULL AS min_standard_attr_id, ov.min_admin_state_up AS min_admin_state_up, ov.avg_standard_attr_id AS avg_standard_attr_id, 'OVS VIF Bound Ports' AS reason FROM OvsBoundPorts AS ov) SELECT * FROM CombinedPortStatusMetrics ORDER BY status, device_owner, reason) SELECT i.status, i.reason, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT b.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ovs_binding, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT spb.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_security_group_binding FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN ports AS p ON p.status = i.status AND (i.device_owner IS NULL OR p.device_owner = i.device_owner) LEFT JOIN ml2_port_bindings AS b ON p.id = b.port_id AND b.vif_type = 'ovs' LEFT JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb ON p.id = spb.port_id GROUP BY i.status, i.reason, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.status, i.device_owner, i.reason
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityAndDns AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortsWithWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER,
