avg_window_min_admin_state_up FROM DownPortsWithWindow AS d GROUP BY d.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT psd.port_id, psd.tenant_id, psd.port_name, psd.security_group_binding_count, psd.dns_record_count, ROUND(CAST(psd.dns_record_count AS REAL) / NULLIF(psd.security_group_binding_count, 0), 2) AS dns_per_security_group_binding_ratio, dpa.down_port_rows, ROUND(dpa.avg_window_min_admin_state_up, 2) AS avg_window_min_admin_state_up FROM PortSecurityAndDns AS psd JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = psd.port_id JOIN DownPortsAgg AS dpa ON dpa.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY dns_per_security_group_binding_ratio DESC, psd.port_id), tenant_agg AS (SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS qualifying_port_count, ROUND(AVG(i.dns_per_security_group_binding_ratio), 2) AS avg_dns_per_sg_binding_ratio FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.tenant_id), avg_across_tenants AS (SELECT AVG(qualifying_port_count) AS avg_qualifying_ports_per_tenant FROM tenant_agg) SELECT ta.tenant_id, ta.qualifying_port_count, ta.avg_dns_per_sg_binding_ratio, aat.avg_qualifying_ports_per_tenant FROM tenant_agg AS ta JOIN avg_across_tenants AS aat ON 1 = 1 ORDER BY ta.qualifying_port_count DESC, ta.tenant_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), q2 AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT q1.NAME AS subnet_name, q1.TENANT_ID, q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.IP_VERSION, q1.CIDR, q1.GATEWAY_IP, q1.ENABLE_DHCP, q1.FIRST_IP AS allocation_first_ip, q1.LAST_IP AS allocation_last_ip, q1.max_standard_attr_id_next2, q2.DESTINATION AS route_destination, q2.NEXTHOP AS route_nexthop, q2.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.NAME = q2.subnet_name AND q1.CIDR = q2.subnet_cidr AND q1.FIRST_IP = q2.FIRST_IP AND q1.LAST_IP = q2.LAST_IP ORDER BY q1.NAME, q2.window_column, q2.DESTINATION) SELECT CASE WHEN LAG(subnet_name) OVER (ORDER BY subnet_name, route_dense_rank_within_subnet, route_destination) = subnet_name THEN NULL ELSE subnet_name END AS subnet_name, tenant_id, network_id, ip_version, cidr, gateway_ip, enable_dhcp, allocation_first_ip, allocation_last_ip, max_standard_attr_id_next2, route_destination, route_nexthop, route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM inner_cte ORDER BY subnet_name, route_dense_rank_within_subnet, route_destination
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), subq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT subq2.PORT_ID) AS total_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT subq1.port_id) AS total_down_ports_with_security_group_binding, AVG(subq2.window_column) AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_avg, MIN(subq1.window_column) AS best_rank_within_security_group_for_down_ports FROM subq2 LEFT JOIN subq1 ON subq2.PORT_ID = subq1.port_id AND subq2.TENANT_ID = subq1.tenant_id GROUP BY subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC) SELECT network_id, vif_type, total_admin_up_ports, total_down_ports_with_security_group_binding, avg_standard_attr_id_window_avg, best_rank_within_security_group_for_down_ports FROM inner_cte WHERE total_admin_up_ports > (SELECT AVG(total_admin_up_ports) FROM inner_cte) ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC

networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.VIF_TYPE, i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.tenant_port_rows, i.avg_window_standard_attr_id, b.avg_tenant_port_rows AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.tenant_port_rows > b.avg_tenant_port_rows * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_ACTIVITY' WHEN i.tenant_port_rows < b.avg_tenant_port_rows * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_ACTIVITY' ELSE 'AVERAGE_ACTIVITY' END AS activity_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN benchmark AS b ORDER BY i.tenant_port_rows DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), TenantUpPortsWithIPsOnNetwork AS (SELECT t.port_id, t.tenant_id, t.mac_address, t.ip_allocation_count, GROUP BY d.NETWORK_ID) SELECT network_id, down_port_name_count, down_port_security_group_count, tenant_up_port_count_with_ips, tenant_up_total_ip_allocations, tenant_up_total_subnet_id_rows, (CAST(tenant_up_port_count_with_ips * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(down_port_name_count, 0)) AS tenant_up_to_down_name_ratio FROM inner_cte WHERE down_port_name_count > 0 AND (CAST(tenant_up_port_count_with_ips * 1.0 AS REAL) / down_port_name_count) > 0.25
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortsWithIPAllocations AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT dpsg.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.port_id) AS down_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT aup.port_id) AS admin_up_port_with_ip_count FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg LEFT JOIN AdminUpPortsWithIPAllocations AS aup ON aup.tenant_id = dpsg.tenant_id GROUP BY dpsg.security_group_id HAVING (CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT aup.port_id) * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.port_id), 0)) > 0.75), PortSubnetCounts AS (SELECT spb.security_group_id, p.id AS port_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ia.subnet_id) AS distinct_subnet_count FROM ports AS p JOIN securitygroupportbindings AS spb ON spb.port_id = p.id JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY spb.security_group_id, p.id) SELECT i.security_group_id, MAX(psc.distinct_subnet_count) AS max_distinct_subnets_on_any_admin_up_port FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PortSubnetCounts AS psc ON psc.security_group_id = i.security_group_id GROUP BY i.security_group_id

p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsWithSg AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), TenantPortCounts AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(p.id) AS tenant_port_count FROM ports AS p WHERE p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT pis.tenant_id, tpc.tenant_port_count, pis.port_id, pis.mac_address, pis.ip_allocation_count, pis.subnet_id_rows, ROUND(CAST(pis.ip_allocation_count AS REAL) / NULLIF(tpc.tenant_port_count, 0), 2) AS ip_allocations_per_tenant_port_ratio, dp.NAME AS down_port_name, dp.NETWORK_ID AS down_port_network_id, dp.STATUS AS down_port_status, dp.DEVICE_OWNER AS down_port_device_owner, dp.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS down_port_security_group_id, dp.window_column AS down_port_window_min_admin_state_up FROM PortIpStats AS pis tenant_ports_to_global_avg_ratio, i.port_id, i.mac_address, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows, i.ip_allocations_per_tenant_port_ratio, i.down_port_name, i.down_port_network_id, WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownNetworkPortDetails AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER, dnpd.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id, COUNT(*) AS matching_down_ports_in_network FROM DownNetworkPortDetails AS dnpd JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.MAC_ADDRESS = dnpd.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.NETWORK_ID = dnpd.NETWORK_ID AND p.DEVICE_OWNER = dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER AND p.STATUS = dnpd.STATUS JOIN PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psa ON psa.port_status = dnpd.STATUS AND psa.device_owner = dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER WHERE p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER, dnpd.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, psa.port_count, psa.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY matching_down_ports_in_network DESC, dnpd.DEVICE_OWNER, dnpd.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT inner_cte.DEVICE_OWNER, inner_cte.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.min_standard_attr_id, inner_cte.matching_down_ports_in_network, avg_stats.avg_matching_down_ports_in_network FROM inner_cte JOIN (SELECT AVG(matching_down_ports_in_network) AS avg_matching_down_ports_in_network FROM inner_cte) AS avg_stats ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH port_sg_dns_summary AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), port_binding_window_details AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbwd.PORT_ID, pbwd.TENANT_ID, psds.port_name, pbwd.HOST, pbwd.VIF_TYPE, pbwd.VNIC_TYPE, pbwd.NETWORK_ID, pbwd.MAC_ADDRESS, pbwd.STATUS, pbwd.DEVICE_OWNER, pbwd.window_column, psds.security_group_binding_count, psds.dns_record_count FROM port_binding_window_details AS pbwd JOIN port_sg_dns_summary AS psds ON psds.port_id = pbwd.PORT_ID AND psds.tenant_id = pbwd.TENANT_ID), avg_sg_bindings AS (SELECT AVG(security_group_binding_count) AS avg_security_group_binding_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.PORT_ID, i.TENANT_ID, i.port_name, i.HOST, i.VIF_TYPE, i.VNIC_TYPE, i.NETWORK_ID, i.MAC_ADDRESS, i.STATUS, i.DEVICE_OWNER, i.window_column, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, (i.security_group_binding_count - a.avg_security_group_binding_count) AS deviation_from_avg_security_group_binding_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN avg_sg_bindings AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownPortsWithSG AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), DownPortsSGCounts AS (SELECT dp.STATUS AS port_status, dp.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_network_with_sg FROM DownPortsWithSG AS dp GROUP BY dp.STATUS, dp.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psoa.port_status, psoa.device_owner, psoa.port_count, psoa.earliest_standard_attr_update, dpsc.down_ports_in_network_with_sg FROM PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psoa JOIN DownPortsSGCounts AS dpsc ON psoa.port_status = dpsc.port_status AND psoa.device_owner = dpsc.device_owner ORDER BY psoa.port_count DESC), AvgPortCount AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.down_ports_in_network_with_sg, (i.port_count - a.avg_port_count) AS deviation_from_avg_port_count FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgPortCount AS a ORDER BY i.port_count DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), subq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT subq2.PORT_ID) AS total_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT subq1.port_id) AS total_down_ports_with_security_group_binding, AVG(subq2.window_column) AS avg_standard_attr_id_window_avg, MIN(subq1.window_column) AS best_rank_within_security_group_for_down_ports FROM subq2 LEFT JOIN subq1 ON subq2.PORT_ID = subq1.port_id AND subq2.TENANT_ID = subq1.tenant_id GROUP BY subq2.NETWORK_ID, subq2.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC) SELECT network_id, MAX(total_admin_up_ports) AS max_admin_up_ports_across_vif_types, AVG(avg_standard_attr_id_window_avg) AS avg_of_viftype_window_avgs, MIN(best_rank_within_security_group_for_down_ports) AS best_rank_among_down_ports_in_network FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id ORDER BY max_admin_up_ports_across_vif_types DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownPortsWithSG AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), DownPortsSGCounts AS (SELECT dp.STATUS AS port_status, dp.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_network_with_sg FROM DownPortsWithSG AS dp GROUP BY dp.STATUS, dp.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT psoa.port_status, psoa.device_owner, psoa.port_count, psoa.earliest_standard_attr_update, dpsc.down_ports_in_network_with_sg FROM PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psoa JOIN DownPortsSGCounts AS dpsc ON psoa.port_status = dpsc.port_status AND psoa.device_owner = dpsc.device_owner ORDER BY psoa.port_count DESC) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, inner_cte.down_ports_in_network_with_sg, (CAST(inner_cte.down_ports_in_network_with_sg * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(inner_cte.port_count, 0)) AS down_sg_ratio FROM inner_cte JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.DEVICE_OWNER = inner_cte.device_owner AND p2.STATUS = inner_cte.port_status WHERE (CAST(inner_cte.down_ports_in_network_with_sg * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(inner_cte.port_count, 0)) > 0.25 GROUP BY inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, inner_cte.port_count, inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update, inner_cte.down_ports_in_network_with_sg ORDER BY down_sg_ratio DESC, inner_cte.port_count DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT n.network_id, n.network_name, n.tenant_id, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at AS network_standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, p.PORT_ID AS port_id, p.HOST AS port_host, p.VIF_TYPE AS port_vif_type, p.VNIC_TYPE AS port_vnic_type, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS port_mac_address, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS port_device_owner, p.window_column AS port_avg_standard_attr_id_window FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS n JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id AND p.TENANT_ID = n.tenant_id ORDER BY n.network_name, p.PORT_ID) SELECT i.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT i.network_id) AS total_networks, COUNT(DISTINCT i.port_id) AS total_ports, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN i.port_vif_type = 'ovs' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ovs_vif_type FROM inner_cte AS i GROUP BY i.tenant_id ORDER BY total_ports DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), IpAllocationsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT sgd.port_id, sgd.tenant_id, sgd.port_name, ipa.mac_address, sgd.security_group_binding_count, sgd.dns_record_count, ipa.ip_allocation_count, ipa.subnet_id_rows FROM SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS sgd JOIN IpAllocationsByPort AS ipa ON ipa.port_id = sgd.port_id AND ipa.tenant_id = sgd.tenant_id) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.port_name, i.mac_address, i.security_group_binding_count, i.dns_record_count, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows, avg_ip.avg_ip_allocations_per_port FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocations_per_port FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) AS avg_ip ON avg_ip.tenant_id = i.tenant_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH active_networks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), tenant_port_ip_summary AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS admin_up_port_count, SUM(p.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, SUM(p.subnet_id_rows) AS total_subnet_id_rows FROM admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocs AS p GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT an.tenant_id, COUNT(an.network_id) AS active_network_count, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.total_ip_allocations, tps.total_subnet_id_rows FROM active_networks AS an JOIN tenant_port_ip_summary AS tps ON tps.tenant_id = an.tenant_id WHERE an.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.total_ip_allocations, tps.total_subnet_id_rows) SELECT tenant_id, active_network_count, latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at, admin_up_port_count, total_ip_allocations, total_subnet_id_rows, (CAST(total_subnet_id_rows * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(total_ip_allocations, 0)) AS subnet_id_row_utilization_pct FROM inner_cte WHERE total_ip_allocations > 0 AND (CAST(total_subnet_id_rows * 1.0 AS REAL) / total_ip_allocations) > 0.75
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBindingPortStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT psgs.port_status, psgs.device_owner, psgs.port_count, psgs.min_standard_attr_id, obs.min_admin_state_up, obs.avg_standard_attr_id FROM PortSecurityGroupStats AS psgs JOIN OvsBindingPortStats AS obs ON psgs.port_status = obs.STATUS) SELECT i.device_owner, i.port_status, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT b2.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_ovs_bound_ports FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.DEVICE_OWNER = i.device_owner AND p2.STATUS = i.port_status LEFT JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b2 ON b2.PORT_ID = p2.ID AND b2.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' GROUP BY i.device_owner, i.port_status, i.port_count, i.min_standard_attr_id, i.min_admin_state_up, i.avg_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.port_count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.STATUS AS port_status, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update, COUNT(sq2.PORT_ID) AS tenant_port_rows, AVG(sq2.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.STATUS = sq1.port_status AND sq2.DEVICE_OWNER = sq1.device_owner WHERE NOT sq2.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.STATUS, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY tenant_port_rows DESC) SELECT i.VIF_TYPE, i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.tenant_port_rows, i.avg_window_standard_attr_id, CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN sa2.UPDATED_AT IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_missing_standard_attr_updated_at FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN PORTS AS p2 ON p2.STATUS = i.port_status AND p2.DEVICE_OWNER = i.device_owner JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = p2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AND sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY i.VIF_TYPE, i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, i.tenant_port_rows, i.avg_window_standard_attr_id ORDER BY i.tenant_port_rows DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), DownPortsWithSecGroup AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT psa.port_status, psa.device_owner, psa.port_count, psa.earliest_standard_attr_update, dp.NAME AS down_port_name, dp.NETWORK_ID AS down_port_network_id, dp.MAC_ADDRESS AS down_port_mac_address, dp.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS down_port_admin_state_up, dp.STATUS AS down_port_status, dp.DEVICE_OWNER AS down_port_device_owner, dp.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS down_port_security_group_id, dp.window_column AS down_port_window_min_admin_state_up FROM PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psa CROSS JOIN DownPortsWithSecGroup AS dp) SELECT inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner, MAX(inner_cte.port_count) AS port_count, MIN(inner_cte.earliest_standard_attr_update) AS earliest_standard_attr_update, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT inner_cte.down_port_security_group_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS any_down_ports_have_security_group FROM inner_cte GROUP BY inner_cte.port_status, inner_cte.device_owner ORDER BY port_count DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH q1 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), q2 AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT q1.NAME AS subnet_name, q1.TENANT_ID, q1.NETWORK_ID, q1.IP_VERSION, q1.CIDR, q1.GATEWAY_IP, q1.ENABLE_DHCP, q1.FIRST_IP AS allocation_first_ip, q1.LAST_IP AS allocation_last_ip, q1.max_standard_attr_id_next2, q2.DESTINATION AS route_destination, q2.NEXTHOP AS route_nexthop, q2.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM q1 JOIN q2 ON q1.NAME = q2.subnet_name AND q1.CIDR = q2.subnet_cidr AND q1.FIRST_IP = q2.FIRST_IP AND q1.LAST_IP = q2.LAST_IP ORDER BY q1.NAME, q2.window_column, q2.DESTINATION), route_stats AS (SELECT s.id AS subnet_id, COUNT(DISTINCT r.destination OR '|' OR r.nexthop) AS total_distinct_routes, CASE WHEN COUNT(r.subnet_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_routes FROM subnets AS s LEFT JOIN subnetroutes AS r ON r.subnet_id = s.id WHERE s.ip_version = 4 AND s.id IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48') GROUP BY s.id) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.ip_version, i.cidr, i.gateway_ip, i.enable_dhcp, i.allocation_first_ip, i.allocation_last_ip, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2, i.route_destination, i.route_nexthop, i.route_dense_rank_within_subnet, rs.has_any_routes, rs.total_distinct_routes FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN subnets AS s ON s.name = i.subnet_name AND s.cidr = i.cidr AND s.ip_version = i.ip_version JOIN ipallocationpools AS p ON p.subnet_id = s.id AND p.first_ip = i.allocation_first_ip AND p.last_ip = i.allocation_last_ip JOIN route_stats AS rs ON rs.subnet_id = s.id ORDER BY i.subnet_name, i.route_dense_rank_within_subnet, i.route_destination
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownTenantPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPortsWithVifWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT aup.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(*) AS admin_up_port_count, COUNT(dtp.port_id) AS down_port_count, (CAST(COUNT(dtp.port_id) * 1.0 AS REAL) / COUNT(*)) AS down_share_of_admin_up_ports FROM AdminUpTenantPortsWithVifWindow AS aup LEFT JOIN DownTenantPortsBySecurityGroup AS dtp ON dtp.port_id = aup.PORT_ID GROUP BY aup.VIF_TYPE HAVING (CAST(COUNT(dtp.port_id) * 1.0 AS REAL) / COUNT(*)) > 0.25) SELECT i.VIF_TYPE, i.admin_up_port_count, i.down_port_count, i.down_share_of_admin_up_ports, CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT sgpb.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_security_group_binding_among_admin_up_ports FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON b.VIF_TYPE = i.VIF_TYPE JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.id = b.port_id AND p.tenant_id = '98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57' AND p.admin_state_up = 1 LEFT JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.port_id = p.id GROUP BY i.VIF_TYPE, i.admin_up_port_count, i.down_port_count, i.down_share_of_admin_up_ports ORDER BY i.down_share_of_admin_up_ports DESC
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH TenantAdminUpBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownNetworkPortsWithWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), DownNetworkPortsWithWindowAndId AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, q.NAME, q.NETWORK_ID, q.MAC_ADDRESS, q.ADMIN_STATE_UP, q.STATUS, q.DEVICE_OWNER, q.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, q.window_column FROM DownNetworkPortsWithWindow AS q JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.NAME = q.NAME AND p.NETWORK_ID = q.NETWORK_ID AND p.MAC_ADDRESS = q.MAC_ADDRESS AND p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = q.ADMIN_STATE_UP AND p.STATUS = q.STATUS AND p.DEVICE_OWNER = q.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT d.port_id, t.tenant_id, t.port_name, d.network_id, d.mac_address, d.admin_state_up, d.status, d.device_owner, d.security_group_id, d.window_column, t.security_group_binding_count, t.dns_record_count FROM DownNetworkPortsWithWindowAndId AS d JOIN TenantAdminUpBoundPorts AS t ON d.port_id = t.port_id ORDER BY d.network_id, d.port_id, d.security_group_id) SELECT network_id, MAX(security_group_binding_count) AS max_security_group_bindings_on_any_port FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetPoolAndMaxAttrNext2 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetAttrDenseRankWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sp.NAME AS subnet_name, sp.TENANT_ID, sp.NETWORK_ID, sp.IP_VERSION, sp.CIDR, sp.GATEWAY_IP, sp.ENABLE_DHCP, sp.FIRST_IP, sp.LAST_IP, sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sadr.window_column AS standard_attr_dense_rank_window FROM SubnetPoolAndMaxAttrNext2 AS sp JOIN SubnetAttrDenseRankWindow AS sadr ON sp.NAME = sadr.NAME) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.ip_version, i.cidr, i.gateway_ip, i.enable_dhcp, i.first_ip, i.last_ip, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2, i.standard_attr_dense_rank_window, tenant_subnets.total_ipv4_subnets, avg_subnets.avg_ipv4_subnets_per_tenant FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN (SELECT tenant_id, COUNT(id) AS total_ipv4_subnets FROM subnets WHERE ip_version = 4 GROUP BY tenant_id) AS tenant_subnets ON tenant_subnets.tenant_id = i.tenant_id JOIN (SELECT AVG(subnet_count) AS avg_ipv4_subnets_per_tenant FROM (SELECT COUNT(id) AS subnet_count FROM subnets WHERE ip_version = 4 GROUP BY tenant_id) AS per_tenant) AS avg_subnets ON 1 = 1
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), ActiveNetworkRbacSummary AS (SELECT name, status, admin_state_up, action, target_tenant, MAX(window_column) AS window_column FROM ActiveNetworkRbacDetails GROUP BY name, status, admin_state_up, action, target_tenant) SELECT anrs.name AS network_name, anrs.status AS network_status, anrs.admin_state_up, anrs.action AS rbac_action, anrs.target_tenant, anrs.window_column, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM ActiveNetworkRbacSummary AS anrs CROSS JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos ORDER BY anrs.name, anrs.action, anrs.target_tenant, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner), AvgPortCount AS (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.network_name, i.network_status, i.admin_state_up, i.rbac_action, i.target_tenant, i.window_column, i.port_status, i.device_owner, i.port_count, i.earliest_standard_attr_update, a.avg_port_count AS benchmark, CASE WHEN i.port_count > a.avg_port_count * 1.5 THEN 'HIGH_USER' WHEN i.port_count < a.avg_port_count * 0.5 THEN 'LOW_USER' ELSE 'AVERAGE_USER' END AS user_category FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AvgPortCount AS a
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetPoolAndMaxAttrNext2 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetAttrDenseRankWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sp.NAME AS subnet_name, sp.TENANT_ID, sp.NETWORK_ID, sp.IP_VERSION, sp.CIDR, sp.GATEWAY_IP, sp.ENABLE_DHCP, sp.FIRST_IP, sp.LAST_IP, sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sadr.window_column AS standard_attr_dense_rank_window FROM SubnetPoolAndMaxAttrNext2 AS sp JOIN SubnetAttrDenseRankWindow AS sadr ON sp.NAME = sadr.NAME) SELECT i.subnet_name, i.TENANT_ID, i.NETWORK_ID, i.IP_VERSION, i.CIDR, i.GATEWAY_IP, i.ENABLE_DHCP, i.FIRST_IP, i.LAST_IP, i.max_standard_attr_id_next2, i.standard_attr_dense_rank_window, sa.RESOURCE_TYPE AS standard_attr_resource_type, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON s.NAME = i.subnet_name AND s.TENANT_ID = i.TENANT_ID AND s.NETWORK_ID = i.NETWORK_ID JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > (SELECT AVG(s2.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) FROM SUBNETS AS s2 WHERE s2.IP_VERSION = 4)

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH SubnetPools AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetAttrDenseRank AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sp.NAME AS subnet_name, sp.TENANT_ID, sp.NETWORK_ID, sp.IP_VERSION, sp.CIDR, sp.GATEWAY_IP, sp.ENABLE_DHCP, sp.FIRST_IP AS allocation_pool_first_ip, sp.LAST_IP AS allocation_pool_last_ip, sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sadr.window_column AS standard_attr_dense_rank_window, ROUND(CAST(sadr.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, 0), 4) AS dense_rank_to_max_attr_ratio FROM SubnetPools AS sp JOIN SubnetAttrDenseRank AS sadr ON sp.NAME = sadr.NAME ORDER BY dense_rank_to_max_attr_ratio DESC) SELECT network_id, MAX(dense_rank_to_max_attr_ratio) AS max_dense_rank_to_max_attr_ratio FROM inner_cte GROUP BY network_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkPortRollup AS (SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, COUNT(DISTINCT autp.PORT_ID) AS total_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT autp.VIF_TYPE) AS distinct_vif_types, AVG(autp.window_column) AS avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn LEFT JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS autp ON autp.NETWORK_ID = atn.network_id AND autp.TENANT_ID = atn.tenant_id GROUP BY atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column) SELECT tenant_id, network_id, network_name, admin_state_up, mtu, standard_attr_updated_at, network_row_number_within_tenant, total_admin_up_ports, distinct_vif_types, avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM NetworkPortRollup ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC, network_name ASC), TenantActiveAvg AS (SELECT tenant_id, AVG(total_admin_up_ports) AS avg_admin_up_ports_per_active_network FROM inner_cte GROUP BY tenant_id) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.network_name, i.admin_state_up, i.mtu, i.standard_attr_updated_at, i.network_row_number_within_tenant, i.total_admin_up_ports, i.distinct_vif_types, i.avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN TenantActiveAvg AS taa ON taa.tenant_id = i.tenant_id WHERE i.total_admin_up_ports > taa.avg_admin_up_ports_per_active_network ORDER BY i.total_admin_up_ports DESC, i.network_name ASC

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH DownPortsWithWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), SecurityGroupGeomMean AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, EXP(AVG(LN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID))) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID > 0 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY geometric_mean_standard_attr_id DESC) SELECT dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count_in_network, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_window_min_admin_state_up, MAX(sgg.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id) AS geometric_mean_standard_attr_id_for_tenant FROM DownPortsWithWindow AS dpw LEFT JOIN SecurityGroupGeomMean AS sgg ON dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sgg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID GROUP BY dpw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY down_port_count_in_network DESC), tenant_down_presence AS (SELECT s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS tenant_down_ports FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d') AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, i.down_port_count_in_network, i.avg_window_min_admin_state_up, i.geometric_mean_standard_attr_id_for_tenant, CASE WHEN COALESCE(tdp.tenant_down_ports, 0) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS has_any_down_ports_in_tenant FROM inner_cte AS i LEFT JOIN tenant_down_presence AS tdp ON i.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = tdp.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY i.down_port_count_in_network DESC LIMIT 10
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortSecurityStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT pss.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT pss.port_id) AS admin_up_port_count, SUM(pss.binding_count) AS total_binding_count, SUM(pss.port_security_enabled_total) AS total_port_security_enabled_across_bindings, ROUND(CAST(SUM(pss.port_security_enabled_total) AS REAL) / NULLIF(SUM(pss.binding_count), 0), 2) AS port_security_enabled_ratio, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_down_port_window_sum FROM PortSecurityStats AS pss LEFT JOIN DownPortWindow AS dpw ON dpw.port_id = pss.port_id AND dpw.tenant_id = pss.tenant_id GROUP BY pss.tenant_id ORDER BY port_security_enabled_ratio DESC) SELECT i.tenant_id, i.admin_up_port_count, i.total_binding_count, i.total_port_security_enabled_across_bindings, i.port_security_enabled_ratio, i.avg_down_port_window_sum, COUNT(p_all.id) AS total_ports_for_tenant, avg_ports.avg_port_count AS avg_ports_across_all_tenants FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN ports AS p_all ON p_all.tenant_id = i.tenant_id, (SELECT AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count FROM (SELECT COUNT(id) AS port_count FROM ports GROUP BY tenant_id) AS sub) AS avg_ports GROUP BY i.tenant_id, i.admin_up_port_count, i.total_binding_count, i.total_port_security_enabled_across_bindings, i.port_security_enabled_ratio, i.avg_down_port_window_sum, avg_ports.avg_port_count
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortIpAllocStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), PortBindingWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbws.PORT_ID AS port_id, pbws.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, pbws.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, pbws.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, pbws.HOST AS host, pbws.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, pbws.VNIC_TYPE AS vnic_type, pbws.STATUS AS status, pbws.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, pbws.window_column AS avg_standard_attr_id_window, pias.ip_allocation_count AS ip_allocation_count, pias.subnet_id_rows AS subnet_id_rows FROM PortBindingWindowStats AS pbws JOIN PortIpAllocStats AS pias ON pias.port_id = pbws.PORT_ID ORDER BY pbws.VIF_TYPE, pbws.PORT_ID), VifTypeAverages AS (SELECT vif_type, AVG(ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocations FROM inner_cte GROUP BY vif_type) SELECT i.port_id, i.tenant_id, i.network_id, i.mac_address, i.host, i.vif_type, i.vnic_type, i.status, i.device_owner, i.avg_standard_attr_id_window, i.ip_allocation_count, i.subnet_id_rows FROM inner_cte AS i JOIN VifTypeAverages AS vta ON vta.vif_type = i.vif_type WHERE i.ip_allocation_count > vta.avg_ip_allocations ORDER BY i.vif_type, i.port_id
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkDownPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), CombinedDownPorts AS (SELECT t.port_id AS port_identifier, t.tenant_id AS scope_id, t.mac_address, t.window_column AS window_metric, 'Tenant Filter' AS reason FROM TenantDownPorts AS t UNION ALL SELECT n.NAME AS port_identifier, n.NETWORK_ID AS scope_id, n.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, n.window_column AS window_metric, 'Network Filter' AS reason FROM NetworkDownPorts AS n) SELECT * FROM CombinedDownPorts ORDER BY reason, scope_id, port_identifier), ReasonCounts AS (SELECT reason, COUNT(*) AS total_ports FROM inner_cte GROUP BY reason) SELECT reason, total_ports FROM ReasonCounts WHERE total_ports > (SELECT AVG(total_ports) FROM ReasonCounts) ORDER BY total_ports DESC, reason
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq1.port_status, sq1.port_count, sq1.min_standard_attr_id, COUNT(DISTINCT sq2.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count, AVG(sq2.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.DEVICE_OWNER = sq1.device_owner GROUP BY sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq1.port_status, sq1.port_count, sq1.min_standard_attr_id ORDER BY admin_up_port_count DESC) SELECT VIF_TYPE, MAX(admin_up_port_count) AS max_admin_up_port_count FROM inner_cte GROUP BY VIF_TYPE

WITH inner_cte AS (WITH TenantDownPorts AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkDownPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), CombinedDownPorts AS (SELECT t.port_id AS port_identifier, t.tenant_id AS scope_id, t.mac_address, t.window_column AS window_metric, 'Tenant Filter' AS reason FROM TenantDownPorts AS t UNION ALL SELECT n.NAME AS port_identifier, n.NETWORK_ID AS scope_id, n.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, n.window_column AS window_metric, 'Network Filter' AS reason FROM NetworkDownPorts AS n) SELECT * FROM CombinedDownPorts ORDER BY reason, scope_id, port_identifier), AverageWindowMetric AS (SELECT AVG(window_metric) AS avg_window_metric FROM inner_cte) SELECT i.port_identifier, i.scope_id, i.mac_address, i.window_metric, i.reason, (i.window_metric - a.avg_window_metric) AS deviation_from_avg_window_metric FROM inner_cte AS i CROSS JOIN AverageWindowMetric AS a ORDER BY i.reason, i.scope_id, i.port_identifier
WITH inner_cte AS (WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), ActiveNetworkRbacSummary AS (SELECT name, status, admin_state_up, action, target_tenant, MAX(window_column) AS window_column FROM ActiveNetworkRbacDetails GROUP BY name, status, admin_state_up, action, target_tenant) SELECT anrs.name AS network_name, anrs.status AS network_status, anrs.admin_state_up, anrs.action AS rbac_action, anrs.target_tenant, anrs.window_column, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM ActiveNetworkRbacSummary AS anrs CROSS JOIN PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos ORDER BY anrs.name, anrs.action, anrs.target_tenant, psos.port_status, psos.device_owner), StatusAverages AS (SELECT port_status, AVG(port_count) AS avg_port_count_for_status FROM inner_cte GROUP BY port_status) SELECT i.network_name, i.network_status, i.admin_state_up, sa.avg_port_count_for_status FROM StatusAverages AS sa WHERE sa.port_status = i.port_status) ORDER BY i.network_name, i.rbac_action, i.target_tenant, i.port_status, i.device_owner
WITH port_status_owner_stats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), subnet_pool_window AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), subnets_matching_ports_standardattrs AS (SELECT COUNT(*) AS matching_subnet_rows_count FROM subnet_pool_window AS spw JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa2 ON sa2.ID = spw.max_standard_attr_id_next2 WHERE sa2.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports') SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM port_status_owner_stats) AS port_status_owner_group_count, smp.matching_subnet_rows_count FROM subnets_matching_ports_standardattrs AS smp
WITH AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT aup.TENANT_ID, AVG(aup.window_column) AS Avg_AdminUp_StdAttr_Window, COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.security_group_id) AS Down_Security_Group_Count, CASE WHEN COUNT(dpsg.port_id) > 0 THEN 'Yes' ELSE 'No' END AS Has_Down_Ports FROM AdminUpPorts AS aup LEFT JOIN DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg ON dpsg.tenant_id = aup.TENANT_ID WHERE aup.STATUS = 'DOWN' GROUP BY aup.TENANT_ID HAVING AVG(aup.window_column) > 100 ORDER BY Avg_AdminUp_StdAttr_Window DESC
WITH HaprxoyLBCountsByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, COUNT(*) AS total_loadbalancers FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING COUNT(*) > 1), ActiveLBBytesInByOpStatus AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT h.OPERATING_STATUS, h.total_loadbalancers, a.total_bytes_in FROM HaprxoyLBCountsByOpStatus AS h JOIN ActiveLBBytesInByOpStatus AS a ON h.OPERATING_STATUS = a.OPERATING_STATUS ORDER BY a.total_bytes_in DESC

WITH PortBindingsWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, AVG(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS), SecurityGroupPortRank AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), TenantDownPortSummary AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS down_port_count, AVG(p.admin_state_up) AS avg_admin_state_up_down FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT sgpr.tenant_id, tds.down_port_count, ROUND(tds.avg_admin_state_up_down, 2) AS avg_admin_state_up_down, sgpr.security_group_id, sgpr.port_id, pbw.NAME AS port_name, sgpr.mac_address, pbw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, pbw.HOST AS binding_host, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.window_column AS avg_admin_state_up_viftype_mac_window, sgpr.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, ROUND(CAST(pbw.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(tds.avg_admin_state_up_down, 0), 2) AS normalized_viftype_window_vs_tenant_avg FROM SecurityGroupPortRank AS sgpr JOIN PortBindingsWindow AS pbw ON pbw.MAC_ADDRESS = sgpr.mac_address JOIN TenantDownPortSummary AS tds ON tds.tenant_id = sgpr.tenant_id ORDER BY normalized_viftype_window_vs_tenant_avg DESC, sgpr.security_group_id, sgpr.port_id
WITH PortSecurityAndDNS AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT d.port_id, d.tenant_id, d.port_name, d.network_id, d.mac_address, d.device_owner, d.ip_address, d.window_column, psd.security_group_binding_count, psd.dns_record_count, ROUND(CAST(psd.dns_record_count AS REAL) / NULLIF(psd.security_group_binding_count, 0), 2) AS dns_records_per_security_group_binding FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN PortSecurityAndDNS AS psd ON psd.port_id = d.port_id ORDER BY dns_records_per_security_group_binding DESC, d.network_id, d.window_column
WITH PortSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN'), PortBindingRank AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT pbr.port_id, pbr.port_name, pbr.mac_address, pbr.binding_host, pbr.vif_type, pbr.window_column AS rank_within_down_status_by_port_id, psw.window_column AS dense_rank_by_port_security_with_2_preceding_frame FROM PortBindingRank AS pbr JOIN PortSecurityWindow AS psw ON pbr.port_id = psw.port_id ORDER BY pbr.port_id
WITH ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE'), AdminUpPorts AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1), TenantPortStats AS (SELECT p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS admin_up_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT p.MAC_ADDRESS) AS unique_mac_count FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 GROUP BY p.TENANT_ID) SELECT an.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id) AS active_networks, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.unique_mac_count, MIN(aup.mac_address) AS min_admin_up_port_mac_address, MAX(aup.mac_address) AS max_admin_up_port_mac_address FROM ActiveNetworks AS an LEFT JOIN TenantPortStats AS tps ON tps.tenant_id = an.tenant_id LEFT JOIN AdminUpPorts AS aup ON 1 = 1 GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.unique_mac_count ORDER BY active_networks DESC, an.tenant_id
WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), DownPortsWithSecurityWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpi.port_id, dpi.tenant_id, dpi.port_name, dpi.network_id, dpi.mac_address, dpi.device_owner, dpi.ip_address, dpi.window_column AS row_num_within_network, dpsw.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, dpsw.ADMIN_STATE_UP, dpsw.STATUS, dpsw.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_window FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS dpi JOIN DownPortsWithSecurityWindow AS dpsw ON dpi.network_id = dpsw.NETWORK_ID AND dpi.port_name = dpsw.NAME AND dpi.mac_address = dpsw.MAC_ADDRESS AND dpi.device_owner = dpsw.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY dpi.network_id, dpi.port_id
WITH shared_subnets AS (SELECT s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, n.TARGET_TENANT AS rbac_target_tenant, SUM(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY s.IP_VERSION ORDER BY s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN NETWORKRBACS AS n ON n.OBJECT_ID = s.NETWORK_ID WHERE n.ACTION = 'access_as_shared'), ipv4_dense_ranks AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT ss.subnet_name, ss.subnet_cidr, ss.rbac_target_tenant, ss.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_ids_ipv_group, idr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_updated_at_ipv4 FROM shared_subnets AS ss JOIN ipv4_dense_ranks AS idr ON idr.NAME = ss.subnet_name
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), sq2 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY psb.port_security_enabled ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN') SELECT sq1.port_id, sq1.tenant_id, sq1.mac_address, sq1.ip_allocation_count, sq1.subnet_id_rows, sq2.window_column FROM sq1 JOIN sq2 ON sq1.port_id = sq2.port_id ORDER BY sq1.ip_allocation_count DESC
WITH HapLoadBalancers AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.ID, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, lb.OPERATING_STATUS, AVG(lb.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY lb.NAME) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), OperatingStatusBytesIn AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT hlb.NAME, hlb.ID, hlb.VIP_ADDRESS, hlb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, hlb.OPERATING_STATUS, hlb.window_column, osbi.total_bytes_in FROM HapLoadBalancers AS hlb JOIN OperatingStatusBytesIn AS osbi ON hlb.OPERATING_STATUS = osbi.OPERATING_STATUS WHERE hlb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' ORDER BY hlb.NAME
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), sq2 AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.NETWORK_ID AS network_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.NETWORK_ID ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), top_networks AS (SELECT network_id, COUNT(DISTINCT port_id) AS down_port_count FROM sq2 GROUP BY network_id ORDER BY down_port_count DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT tn.network_id, tn.down_port_count, sq2.port_id, sq2.tenant_id, sq2.port_name, sq2.mac_address, sq2.device_owner, sq2.ip_address, sq2.window_column AS port_row_number_within_network, sq1.binding_host, sq1.vif_type, sq1.window_column AS port_rank_within_down_status FROM top_networks AS tn JOIN sq2 ON tn.network_id = sq2.network_id LEFT JOIN sq1 ON sq2.port_id = sq1.port_id ORDER BY tn.down_port_count DESC, tn.network_id, sq2.window_column, sq2.port_id
WITH PortWindowSums AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59'), AdminUpPortSecurityAgg AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT pws.PORT_ID, pws.TENANT_ID, pws.MAC_ADDRESS, pws.window_column, aupsa.binding_count, aupsa.port_security_enabled_total FROM PortWindowSums AS pws JOIN AdminUpPortSecurityAgg AS aupsa ON pws.PORT_ID = aupsa.port_id AND pws.TENANT_ID = aupsa.tenant_id AND pws.MAC_ADDRESS = aupsa.mac_address
WITH DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN'), AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS (SELECT p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, psb.PORT_SECURITY_ENABLED AS port_security_enabled, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY p.MAC_ADDRESS ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1) SELECT dpwb.port_id, dpwb.port_name, dpwb.mac_address, dpwb.binding_host, dpwb.vif_type, dpwb.window_column AS down_status_rank, aupws.port_security_enabled, aupws.window_column AS admin_up_row_number FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dpwb JOIN AdminUpPortsWithSecurity AS aupws ON dpwb.mac_address = aupws.mac_address WHERE aupws.port_security_enabled = 1 ORDER BY dpwb.port_id
WITH HapLoadBalancers AS (SELECT lb.NAME, lb.ID, lb.VIP_ADDRESS, lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, lb.OPERATING_STATUS, AVG(lb.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY pra.PROVIDER_NAME ORDER BY lb.NAME) AS window_column FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN PROVIDERRESOURCEASSOCIATIONS AS pra ON lb.ID = pra.RESOURCE_ID WHERE pra.PROVIDER_NAME = 'haproxy'), OperatingStatusBytesIn AS (SELECT lb.OPERATING_STATUS, SUM(s.BYTES_IN) AS total_bytes_in FROM LBAAS_LOADBALANCERS AS lb JOIN LBAAS_LOADBALANCER_STATISTICS AS s ON lb.ID = s.LOADBALANCER_ID WHERE lb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' GROUP BY lb.OPERATING_STATUS HAVING SUM(s.BYTES_IN) > 1000000) SELECT hlb.NAME, hlb.ID, hlb.VIP_ADDRESS, hlb.PROVISIONING_STATUS, hlb.OPERATING_STATUS, hlb.window_column, osbi.total_bytes_in, ROUND(CAST(osbi.total_bytes_in AS REAL) / NULLIF(hlb.window_column, 0), 2) AS BytesIn_Per_WindowAvgId FROM HapLoadBalancers AS hlb JOIN OperatingStatusBytesIn AS osbi ON hlb.OPERATING_STATUS = osbi.OPERATING_STATUS WHERE hlb.PROVISIONING_STATUS = 'ACTIVE' ORDER BY BytesIn_Per_WindowAvgId DESC, hlb.NAME
WITH PortIpAllocStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), HostVifWindowSums AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT h.PORT_ID AS port_id, h.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, h.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, pias.ip_allocation_count, pias.subnet_id_rows, h.window_column FROM HostVifWindowSums AS h JOIN PortIpAllocStats AS pias ON pias.port_id = h.PORT_ID AND pias.tenant_id = h.TENANT_ID AND pias.mac_address = h.MAC_ADDRESS ORDER BY h.window_column DESC, h.PORT_ID
WITH AdminUpPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsWithBindings AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, b.HOST AS binding_host, b.VIF_TYPE AS vif_type, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY p.STATUS ORDER BY p.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT dpwb.port_id, dpwb.port_name, apwi.tenant_id, dpwb.mac_address, dpwb.binding_host, dpwb.vif_type, apwi.ip_allocation_count, apwi.subnet_id_rows, dpwb.window_column FROM DownPortsWithBindings AS dpwb JOIN AdminUpPortsWithIPs AS apwi ON dpwb.port_id = apwi.port_id ORDER BY dpwb.window_column, dpwb.port_id
WITH AdminUpPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN') SELECT a.port_id, a.tenant_id, a.mac_address, a.ip_allocation_count, a.subnet_id_rows, d.window_column, ROUND(CAST(a.ip_allocation_count AS REAL) / NULLIF(d.window_column, 0), 2) AS IpAllocations_Per_WindowId FROM AdminUpPortsWithIPs AS a JOIN DownPortsWindow AS d ON d.port_id = a.port_id ORDER BY IpAllocations_Per_WindowId DESC
WITH DownPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NAME AS port_name, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, ia.IP_ADDRESS AS ip_address, ia.SUBNET_ID AS subnet_id, AVG(p.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY p.ID ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN IPALLOCATIONS AS ia ON p.ID = ia.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' ORDER BY ia.IP_ADDRESS), Drive59PortsWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, p.TENANT_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE b.HOST = 'drive-59') SELECT d.port_id, d.port_name, d.mac_address, d.ip_address, d.subnet_id, d.window_column AS down_ports_avg_port_id_window, dr.TENANT_ID, dr.window_column AS drive59_running_sum_standard_attr_id_window FROM DownPortsWithIPs AS d JOIN Drive59PortsWindow AS dr ON dr.PORT_ID = d.port_id WHERE d.mac_address = dr.MAC_ADDRESS ORDER BY d.ip_address
WITH SubnetStdAttrWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4), SubnetRoutesPoolsWindow AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4) SELECT srpw.subnet_name, srpw.subnet_cidr, srpw.DESTINATION, srpw.NEXTHOP, srpw.FIRST_IP, srpw.LAST_IP, ssaw.window_column AS std_attr_dense_rank_updated_at_window, srpw.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM SubnetRoutesPoolsWindow AS srpw JOIN SubnetStdAttrWindow AS ssaw ON srpw.subnet_name = ssaw.NAME
