WITH PortSecurityAndDns AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortsWithWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), DownPortsAgg AS (SELECT d.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, COUNT(*) AS down_port_rows, AVG(d.window_column) AS avg_window_min_admin_state_up FROM DownPortsWithWindow AS d GROUP BY d.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT psd.port_id, psd.tenant_id, psd.port_name, psd.security_group_binding_count, psd.dns_record_count, ROUND(CAST(psd.dns_record_count AS REAL) / NULLIF(psd.security_group_binding_count, 0), 2) AS dns_per_security_group_binding_ratio, dpa.down_port_rows, ROUND(dpa.avg_window_min_admin_state_up, 2) AS avg_window_min_admin_state_up FROM PortSecurityAndDns AS psd JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb2 ON sgpb2.PORT_ID = psd.port_id JOIN DownPortsAgg AS dpa ON dpa.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sgpb2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY dns_per_security_group_binding_ratio DESC, psd.port_id
WITH SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), IpAllocationsByPort AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT sgdp.port_id, sgdp.tenant_id, sgdp.port_name, iap.mac_address, sgdp.security_group_binding_count, sgdp.dns_record_count, iap.ip_allocation_count, iap.subnet_id_rows FROM SecurityGroupAndDnsByPort AS sgdp JOIN IpAllocationsByPort AS iap ON sgdp.port_id = iap.port_id AND sgdp.tenant_id = iap.tenant_id WHERE sgdp.security_group_binding_count >= 1 ORDER BY sgdp.security_group_binding_count DESC, iap.ip_allocation_count DESC
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT sq2.NETWORK_ID, sq2.NAME AS port_name, sq2.MAC_ADDRESS, sq2.ADMIN_STATE_UP, sq2.STATUS AS port_status, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq2.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, sq2.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_window, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq1.port_status = sq2.STATUS AND sq1.device_owner = sq2.DEVICE_OWNER ORDER BY sq1.port_count DESC, sq2.NAME
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT n.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT n.network_id) AS active_network_count, COUNT(DISTINCT p.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count, ROUND(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT p.PORT_ID) AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT n.network_id), 0), 2) AS ports_per_active_network_ratio, ROUND(AVG(p.window_column), 2) AS avg_port_window_standard_attr_id, MAX(n.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS n JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS p ON p.tenant_id = n.tenant_id AND p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id WHERE n.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY n.tenant_id ORDER BY ports_per_active_network_ratio DESC
WITH SubnetDetails AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetRouteDetails AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sd.NAME AS subnet_name, sd.TENANT_ID, sd.NETWORK_ID, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP AS allocation_first_ip, sd.LAST_IP AS allocation_last_ip, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2, srd.DESTINATION AS route_destination, srd.NEXTHOP AS route_nexthop, srd.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM SubnetDetails AS sd LEFT JOIN SubnetRouteDetails AS srd ON sd.NAME = srd.subnet_name AND sd.CIDR = srd.subnet_cidr AND sd.FIRST_IP = srd.FIRST_IP AND sd.LAST_IP = srd.LAST_IP ORDER BY sd.NAME, srd.DESTINATION
WITH Subq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS), Subq2 AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT s1.STATUS AS port_status, s1.min_admin_state_up AS ovs_device_min_admin_state_up, s1.avg_standard_attr_id AS ovs_device_avg_standard_attr_id, COUNT(*) AS down_ports_in_network_with_security_group_count, AVG(s2.window_column) AS avg_window_min_admin_state_up_for_down_ports FROM Subq1 AS s1 CROSS JOIN Subq2 AS s2 GROUP BY s1.STATUS, s1.min_admin_state_up, s1.avg_standard_attr_id
WITH default_external_networks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), port_status_device_owner_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT den.TENANT_ID) AS tenant_count_with_default_external_networks, COUNT(*) AS port_status_device_owner_group_count FROM default_external_networks AS den CROSS JOIN port_status_device_owner_summary AS psdos
WITH subq1 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), subq2 AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT subq1.security_group_id, subq1.port_id AS down_port_id, subq1.tenant_id, subq1.mac_address, subq1.window_column AS down_port_rank_within_security_group, subq2.ip_allocation_count, subq2.subnet_id_rows FROM subq1 LEFT JOIN subq2 ON subq1.port_id = subq2.port_id ORDER BY subq1.security_group_id, subq1.window_column, subq1.port_id
WITH ActiveNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), PortSecurityAndDns AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT an.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id) AS active_network_count, COUNT(DISTINCT psd.port_id) AS admin_up_sg_bound_port_count, ROUND(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT psd.port_id) AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id), 0), 2) AS avg_ports_per_active_network, SUM(psd.security_group_binding_count) AS total_security_group_bindings_across_ports, SUM(psd.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records_across_ports FROM ActiveNetworks AS an CROSS JOIN PortSecurityAndDns AS psd GROUP BY an.tenant_id
WITH PortSecurityAndDNSStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), PortBindingWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbws.PORT_ID AS port_id, pbws.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, psds.port_name, pbws.HOST, pbws.VIF_TYPE, pbws.VNIC_TYPE, pbws.NETWORK_ID, pbws.MAC_ADDRESS, pbws.STATUS, pbws.DEVICE_OWNER, pbws.window_column, psds.security_group_binding_count, psds.dns_record_count FROM PortBindingWindowStats AS pbws JOIN PortSecurityAndDNSStats AS psds ON pbws.PORT_ID = psds.port_id ORDER BY pbws.PORT_ID
WITH default_external_networks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), port_status_owner_stats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), top10_device_owner_port_counts AS (SELECT device_owner, SUM(port_count) AS total_ports FROM port_status_owner_stats GROUP BY device_owner ORDER BY total_ports DESC LIMIT 10) SELECT t.device_owner, t.total_ports, den.NETWORK_ID, den.IS_DEFAULT, den.TENANT_ID, den.NAME AS network_name, den.STATUS AS network_status, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP, den.MTU, den.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AS network_standard_attr_id, den.window_column AS min_mtu_within_tenant, psos.port_status, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM top10_device_owner_port_counts AS t JOIN port_status_owner_stats AS psos ON psos.device_owner = t.device_owner CROSS JOIN default_external_networks AS den ORDER BY t.total_ports DESC, t.device_owner, psos.port_status
WITH PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortStatusOvsBindingStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT psosgs.port_status, psosgs.device_owner, psosgs.port_count, psosgs.min_standard_attr_id, psobs.min_admin_state_up, psobs.avg_standard_attr_id FROM PortStatusOwnerSecurityGroupStats AS psosgs JOIN PortStatusOvsBindingStats AS psobs ON psosgs.port_status = psobs.STATUS ORDER BY psosgs.port_count DESC
WITH PortSecurityGroupRanks AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), DownTenantPortsWithNetwork AS (SELECT psgr.port_id, psgr.tenant_id, psgr.mac_address, psgr.security_group_id, psgr.window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group, p.network_id FROM PortSecurityGroupRanks AS psgr JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.id = psgr.port_id WHERE psgr.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, dtp.port_id, dtp.mac_address, dtp.security_group_id, dtp.port_rank_within_security_group FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn JOIN DownTenantPortsWithNetwork AS dtp ON dtp.network_id = atn.network_id AND dtp.tenant_id = atn.tenant_id ORDER BY atn.tenant_id, atn.network_name, dtp.security_group_id, dtp.port_id
WITH PortSecurityStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT pss.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT pss.port_id) AS admin_up_port_count, SUM(pss.binding_count) AS total_binding_count, SUM(pss.port_security_enabled_total) AS total_port_security_enabled_across_bindings, ROUND(CAST(SUM(pss.port_security_enabled_total) AS REAL) / NULLIF(SUM(pss.binding_count), 0), 2) AS port_security_enabled_ratio, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_down_port_window_sum FROM PortSecurityStats AS pss LEFT JOIN DownPortWindow AS dpw ON dpw.port_id = pss.port_id AND dpw.tenant_id = pss.tenant_id GROUP BY pss.tenant_id ORDER BY port_security_enabled_ratio DESC
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortBindingAndDNSCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortsThatAreAlsoAdminUp AS (SELECT dpsg.port_id, dpsg.tenant_id, dpsg.mac_address, dpsg.security_group_id, dpsg.window_column, aup.port_name, aup.security_group_binding_count, aup.dns_record_count FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg JOIN AdminUpPortBindingAndDNSCounts AS aup ON dpsg.port_id = aup.port_id AND dpsg.tenant_id = aup.tenant_id) SELECT port_id, tenant_id, mac_address, security_group_id, window_column AS port_rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, port_name, security_group_binding_count, dns_record_count FROM DownPortsThatAreAlsoAdminUp ORDER BY security_group_id, port_id
WITH port_status_owner_summary AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), active_network_rbac_details AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), active_network_rbac_counts AS (SELECT action, target_tenant, COUNT(*) AS active_network_rbac_row_count FROM active_network_rbac_details GROUP BY action, target_tenant) SELECT p.port_status, p.device_owner, p.port_count, p.earliest_standard_attr_update, c.action, c.target_tenant, c.active_network_rbac_row_count FROM port_status_owner_summary AS p CROSS JOIN active_network_rbac_counts AS c
WITH PortIpAllocationStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsWindowBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), SecurityGroupCountsForTenant AS (SELECT sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID AS security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN p.STATUS = 'DOWN' THEN p.ID END) AS down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID WHERE p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) SELECT sct.security_group_id, sct.total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant, sct.down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant, AVG(dpw.window_column) AS avg_down_port_running_sum_standard_attr_id, COUNT(DISTINCT pia.port_id) AS admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocations_in_sg, AVG(pia.ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocations_per_admin_up_port_in_sg, MAX(pia.subnet_id_rows) AS max_subnet_id_rows_among_admin_up_ports_in_sg FROM SecurityGroupCountsForTenant AS sct JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb ON sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = sct.security_group_id LEFT JOIN DownPortsWindowBySecurityGroup AS dpw ON dpw.port_id = sgpb.PORT_ID LEFT JOIN PortIpAllocationStats AS pia ON pia.port_id = sgpb.PORT_ID WHERE sct.down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant > 0 AND (sct.total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant - sct.down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant) > 0 GROUP BY sct.security_group_id, sct.total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant, sct.down_ports_in_sg_for_tenant ORDER BY sct.total_ports_in_sg_for_tenant DESC
WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBoundDevicePortStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.earliest_standard_attr_update, obdps.min_admin_state_up, obdps.avg_standard_attr_id FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos JOIN OvsBoundDevicePortStats AS obdps ON psos.port_status = obdps.STATUS ORDER BY psos.port_status, psos.device_owner
WITH PortIpAllocationStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsSecurityGroupWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT dpsgw.tenant_id, dpsgw.port_id, dpsgw.mac_address, dpsgw.window_column, pis.ip_allocation_count, pis.subnet_id_rows FROM DownPortsSecurityGroupWindow AS dpsgw JOIN PortIpAllocationStats AS pis ON pis.port_id = dpsgw.port_id AND pis.tenant_id = dpsgw.tenant_id WHERE pis.ip_allocation_count >= 1 ORDER BY dpsgw.window_column DESC, dpsgw.port_id
WITH SubnetPools AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetAttrDenseRank AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sp.NAME AS subnet_name, sp.TENANT_ID, sp.NETWORK_ID, sp.IP_VERSION, sp.CIDR, sp.GATEWAY_IP, sp.ENABLE_DHCP, sp.FIRST_IP AS allocation_pool_first_ip, sp.LAST_IP AS allocation_pool_last_ip, sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sadr.window_column AS standard_attr_dense_rank_window, ROUND(CAST(sadr.window_column AS REAL) / NULLIF(sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, 0), 4) AS dense_rank_to_max_attr_ratio FROM SubnetPools AS sp JOIN SubnetAttrDenseRank AS sadr ON sp.NAME = sadr.NAME ORDER BY dense_rank_to_max_attr_ratio DESC
WITH sq1 AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), sq2 AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.STATUS AS port_status, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update, COUNT(sq2.PORT_ID) AS tenant_port_rows, AVG(sq2.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM sq2 JOIN sq1 ON sq2.STATUS = sq1.port_status AND sq2.DEVICE_OWNER = sq1.device_owner WHERE NOT sq2.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY sq2.VIF_TYPE, sq2.STATUS, sq2.DEVICE_OWNER, sq1.port_count, sq1.earliest_standard_attr_update ORDER BY tenant_port_rows DESC
WITH ports_rank_within_sg AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), ports_running_sum_std_attr AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT prws.port_id, prws.tenant_id, prws.mac_address, prws.security_group_id, prws.window_column AS rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, prssa.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group FROM ports_rank_within_sg AS prws JOIN ports_running_sum_std_attr AS prssa ON prssa.port_id = prws.port_id AND prssa.tenant_id = prws.tenant_id AND prssa.mac_address = prws.mac_address
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), TenantPortCounts AS (SELECT ap.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT ap.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count FROM AdminUpTenantPorts AS ap GROUP BY ap.TENANT_ID) SELECT an.tenant_id, tpc.admin_up_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT an.network_id) AS active_network_count, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS an JOIN TenantPortCounts AS tpc ON an.tenant_id = tpc.tenant_id WHERE tpc.admin_up_port_count >= 2 GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tpc.admin_up_port_count ORDER BY tpc.admin_up_port_count DESC, active_network_count DESC
WITH RankedPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), RunningSumStandardAttrIdBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT r.port_id, r.tenant_id, r.mac_address, r.security_group_id, r.window_column AS rank_within_security_group_by_port_id, s.window_column AS running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group FROM RankedPortsBySecurityGroup AS r JOIN RunningSumStandardAttrIdBySecurityGroup AS s ON s.port_id = r.port_id AND s.tenant_id = r.tenant_id AND s.mac_address = r.mac_address
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortsByVifType AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT a.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT a.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT d.port_id) AS down_port_count, ROUND(CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT d.port_id) AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT a.PORT_ID), 0), 2) AS down_to_admin_up_port_ratio, AVG(a.window_column) AS avg_admin_up_window_avg_standard_attr_id, AVG(d.window_column) AS avg_down_window_running_sum_standard_attr_id FROM AdminUpPortsByVifType AS a LEFT JOIN DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS d ON d.tenant_id = a.TENANT_ID GROUP BY a.TENANT_ID ORDER BY down_to_admin_up_port_ratio DESC
WITH SubnetPoolsAndMaxStdAttrNext2 AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), SubnetStdAttrDenseRankWindow AS (SELECT s.NAME, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY sa.UPDATED_AT ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS window_column FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sp.NAME AS subnet_name, sp.TENANT_ID, sp.NETWORK_ID, sp.IP_VERSION, sp.CIDR, sp.GATEWAY_IP, sp.ENABLE_DHCP, sp.FIRST_IP, sp.LAST_IP, sp.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sr.window_column AS dense_rank_by_updated_at_window FROM SubnetPoolsAndMaxStdAttrNext2 AS sp JOIN SubnetStdAttrDenseRankWindow AS sr ON sp.NAME = sr.NAME
WITH DefaultExternalNetworks AS (SELECT e.NETWORK_ID, e.IS_DEFAULT, n.TENANT_ID, n.NAME, n.STATUS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.MTU, n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID, MIN(n.MTU) OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.TENANT_ID) AS window_column FROM EXTERNALNETWORKS AS e JOIN NETWORKS AS n ON e.NETWORK_ID = n.ID WHERE e.IS_DEFAULT = 0 AND NOT n.ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), PortsOnDefaultExternalNetworks AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.NETWORK_ID, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner FROM PORTS AS p WHERE p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT NETWORK_ID FROM DefaultExternalNetworks)) SELECT den.NETWORK_ID, den.IS_DEFAULT, den.TENANT_ID, den.NAME AS network_name, den.STATUS AS network_status, den.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS network_admin_state_up, den.MTU, den.STANDARD_ATTR_ID AS network_standard_attr_id, den.window_column AS tenant_min_mtu, psa.port_status, psa.device_owner, psa.port_count, psa.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM DefaultExternalNetworks AS den JOIN PortsOnDefaultExternalNetworks AS pden ON pden.NETWORK_ID = den.NETWORK_ID JOIN PortStatusOwnerAgg AS psa ON psa.port_status = pden.port_status AND psa.device_owner = pden.device_owner ORDER BY den.TENANT_ID, den.NETWORK_ID, psa.port_status, psa.device_owner
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkPortRollup AS (SELECT atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, COUNT(DISTINCT autp.PORT_ID) AS total_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT autp.VIF_TYPE) AS distinct_vif_types, AVG(autp.window_column) AS avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS atn LEFT JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS autp ON autp.NETWORK_ID = atn.network_id AND autp.TENANT_ID = atn.tenant_id GROUP BY atn.tenant_id, atn.network_id, atn.network_name, atn.admin_state_up, atn.mtu, atn.standard_attr_updated_at, atn.window_column) SELECT tenant_id, network_id, network_name, admin_state_up, mtu, standard_attr_updated_at, network_row_number_within_tenant, total_admin_up_ports, distinct_vif_types, avg_port_window_avg_standard_attr_id FROM NetworkPortRollup ORDER BY total_admin_up_ports DESC, network_name ASC
WITH PortSecurityAndDnsCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), DownPortsWindowedAttrSum AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.SECURITY_GROUP_ID ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT psc.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT psc.port_id) AS admin_up_bound_port_count, AVG(psc.security_group_binding_count) AS avg_security_group_bindings_per_admin_up_port, SUM(psc.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_records_on_admin_up_ports, COUNT(DISTINCT dpa.port_id) AS down_bound_port_count, AVG(dpa.window_column) AS avg_running_sum_standard_attr_id_within_security_group_for_down_ports FROM PortSecurityAndDnsCounts AS psc JOIN DownPortsWindowedAttrSum AS dpa ON psc.tenant_id = dpa.tenant_id WHERE psc.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY psc.tenant_id ORDER BY admin_up_bound_port_count DESC
WITH PortSecurityAgg AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsSecurityGroups AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT psa.tenant_id, COUNT(DISTINCT psa.port_id) AS admin_up_ports_with_security_bindings, SUM(psa.binding_count) AS total_port_security_bindings, SUM(psa.port_security_enabled_total) AS total_port_security_enabled_across_bindings, COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) AS distinct_security_groups_on_down_ports_in_network, ROUND(CAST(SUM(psa.port_security_enabled_total) AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.SECURITY_GROUP_ID), 0), 2) AS enabled_per_security_group_ratio FROM PortSecurityAgg AS psa JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = psa.port_id JOIN DownPortsSecurityGroups AS dpsg ON dpsg.NETWORK_ID = p.NETWORK_ID WHERE p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d') AND psa.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY psa.tenant_id ORDER BY enabled_per_security_group_ratio DESC
WITH PortStatusOwnerStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), AdminUpPortWindowStats AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.min_standard_attr_id, aupws.VIF_TYPE, COUNT(DISTINCT aupws.PORT_ID) AS admin_up_ports, AVG(aupws.window_column) AS avg_window_standard_attr_id FROM PortStatusOwnerStats AS psos JOIN AdminUpPortWindowStats AS aupws ON psos.port_status = aupws.STATUS AND psos.device_owner = aupws.DEVICE_OWNER GROUP BY psos.port_status, psos.device_owner, psos.port_count, psos.min_standard_attr_id, aupws.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY psos.port_count DESC
WITH PortSecurityGroupStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBindingPortStats AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS) SELECT psgs.port_status, psgs.device_owner, psgs.port_count, psgs.min_standard_attr_id, obs.min_admin_state_up, obs.avg_standard_attr_id FROM PortSecurityGroupStats AS psgs JOIN OvsBindingPortStats AS obs ON psgs.port_status = obs.STATUS
WITH PortSecurityAgg AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(psb.port_id) AS binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN psb.port_security_enabled THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS port_security_enabled_total FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsWindow AS (SELECT p.ID AS port_id, p.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS mac_address, SUM(sa.ID) OVER (PARTITION BY sa.RESOURCE_TYPE ORDER BY sa.ID) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7')) SELECT psa.port_id, psa.tenant_id, psa.mac_address, psa.binding_count, psa.port_security_enabled_total, dpw.window_column FROM PortSecurityAgg AS psa JOIN DownPortsWindow AS dpw ON psa.port_id = dpw.port_id AND psa.tenant_id = dpw.tenant_id AND psa.mac_address = dpw.mac_address
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortBindingAndDnsCounts AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name) SELECT dpsg.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.port_id) AS down_port_count, MAX(au.security_group_binding_count) AS max_security_group_binding_count_among_admin_up_ports, SUM(au.dns_record_count) AS total_dns_record_count_for_admin_up_ports, ROUND(CAST(SUM(au.dns_record_count) AS REAL) / NULLIF(MAX(au.security_group_binding_count), 0), 2) AS dns_records_per_max_binding_ratio FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg JOIN AdminUpPortBindingAndDnsCounts AS au ON au.port_id = dpsg.port_id AND au.tenant_id = dpsg.tenant_id WHERE dpsg.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY dpsg.security_group_id ORDER BY dns_records_per_max_binding_ratio DESC
WITH PortIPStats AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), PortBindingWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), TenantAvgIPAlloc AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, AVG(ips.ip_allocation_count) AS avg_ip_allocation_count FROM ports AS p JOIN PortIPStats AS ips ON ips.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT pbw.PORT_ID, pbw.TENANT_ID, pbw.NETWORK_ID, pbw.MAC_ADDRESS, pbw.HOST, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.VNIC_TYPE, pbw.STATUS, pbw.DEVICE_OWNER, pbw.window_column, pis.ip_allocation_count, pis.subnet_id_rows FROM PortBindingWindow AS pbw JOIN PortIPStats AS pis ON pis.port_id = pbw.PORT_ID JOIN TenantAvgIPAlloc AS ta ON ta.tenant_id = pbw.TENANT_ID WHERE pis.ip_allocation_count > ta.avg_ip_allocation_count
WITH DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpPortsWithIPAllocations AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address) SELECT dpsg.security_group_id, COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.port_id) AS down_port_count, COUNT(DISTINCT aup.port_id) AS admin_up_port_with_ip_count FROM DownPortsBySecurityGroup AS dpsg LEFT JOIN AdminUpPortsWithIPAllocations AS aup ON aup.tenant_id = dpsg.tenant_id GROUP BY dpsg.security_group_id HAVING (CAST(COUNT(DISTINCT aup.port_id) * 1.0 AS REAL) / NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT dpsg.port_id), 0)) > 0.75
WITH TenantDownPortsBySecurityGroup AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, sgpb.security_group_id, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY sgpb.security_group_id ORDER BY p.id) AS window_column FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.id = psb.port_id WHERE p.status = 'DOWN' AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), NetworkDownPortsWithAdminStateWindow AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT n.NAME, n.NETWORK_ID, n.MAC_ADDRESS, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP, n.STATUS, n.DEVICE_OWNER, n.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, n.window_column AS min_admin_state_up_window_3_preceding_1_following, t.port_id, t.tenant_id, t.window_column AS rank_within_security_group_by_port_id FROM NetworkDownPortsWithAdminStateWindow AS n JOIN TenantDownPortsBySecurityGroup AS t ON n.SECURITY_GROUP_ID = t.security_group_id ORDER BY n.NETWORK_ID, n.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, t.port_id
WITH SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(DISTINCT p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS min_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS spb ON p.ID = spb.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('17ea94ad74b64b9d92f4888336a598c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), OvsBoundPorts AS (SELECT p.STATUS, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) AS min_admin_state_up, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) AS avg_standard_attr_id FROM PORTS AS p JOIN ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b ON p.ID = b.PORT_ID WHERE b.VIF_TYPE = 'ovs' AND p.DEVICE_ID IN ('da811c75-0f9b-462c-b020-8ea55f6ca7c7') GROUP BY p.STATUS), CombinedPortStatusMetrics AS (SELECT sg.port_status AS status, sg.device_owner AS device_owner, sg.port_count AS port_count, sg.min_standard_attr_id AS min_standard_attr_id, NULL AS min_admin_state_up, NULL AS avg_standard_attr_id, 'Security Group Bound Ports' AS reason FROM SecurityGroupBoundPorts AS sg UNION ALL SELECT ov.STATUS AS status, NULL AS device_owner, NULL AS port_count, NULL AS min_standard_attr_id, ov.min_admin_state_up AS min_admin_state_up, ov.avg_standard_attr_id AS avg_standard_attr_id, 'OVS VIF Bound Ports' AS reason FROM OvsBoundPorts AS ov) SELECT * FROM CombinedPortStatusMetrics ORDER BY status, device_owner, reason
WITH TenantUpPortsWithIPs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), DownPortsOnNetworkWithSG AS (SELECT p.NAME, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.ADMIN_STATE_UP, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, s.SECURITY_GROUP_ID, MIN(p.ADMIN_STATE_UP) OVER (ORDER BY p.ID ROWS BETWEEN 3 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM PORTS AS p JOIN SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS s ON p.ID = s.PORT_ID WHERE p.STATUS = 'DOWN' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')), TenantUpPortsWithIPsOnNetwork AS (SELECT t.port_id, t.tenant_id, t.mac_address, t.ip_allocation_count, t.subnet_id_rows FROM TenantUpPortsWithIPs AS t JOIN PORTS AS p ON p.ID = t.port_id WHERE p.NETWORK_ID IN ('0a1d0a27-cffa-4de3-92c5-9d3fd3f2e74d')) SELECT d.NETWORK_ID, COUNT(DISTINCT d.NAME) AS down_port_name_count, COUNT(DISTINCT d.SECURITY_GROUP_ID) AS down_port_security_group_count, COUNT(DISTINCT t.port_id) AS tenant_up_port_count_with_ips, SUM(t.ip_allocation_count) AS tenant_up_total_ip_allocations, SUM(t.subnet_id_rows) AS tenant_up_total_subnet_id_rows FROM DownPortsOnNetworkWithSG AS d LEFT JOIN TenantUpPortsWithIPsOnNetwork AS t ON t.mac_address = d.MAC_ADDRESS GROUP BY d.NETWORK_ID
WITH active_networks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocs AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address, COUNT(ia.ip_address) AS ip_allocation_count, SUM(CASE WHEN NOT ia.subnet_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS subnet_id_rows FROM ports AS p JOIN portsecuritybindings AS psb ON psb.port_id = p.id JOIN ipallocations AS ia ON ia.port_id = p.id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND NOT ia.ip_address IS NULL AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.mac_address), tenant_port_ip_summary AS (SELECT p.tenant_id, COUNT(*) AS admin_up_port_count, SUM(p.ip_allocation_count) AS total_ip_allocations, SUM(p.subnet_id_rows) AS total_subnet_id_rows FROM admin_up_ports_with_ip_allocs AS p GROUP BY p.tenant_id) SELECT an.tenant_id, COUNT(an.network_id) AS active_network_count, MAX(an.standard_attr_updated_at) AS latest_network_standard_attr_updated_at, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.total_ip_allocations, tps.total_subnet_id_rows FROM active_networks AS an JOIN tenant_port_ip_summary AS tps ON tps.tenant_id = an.tenant_id WHERE an.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY an.tenant_id, tps.admin_up_port_count, tps.total_ip_allocations, tps.total_subnet_id_rows
WITH ActiveTenantNetworks AS (SELECT n.ID AS network_id, n.NAME AS network_name, n.TENANT_ID AS tenant_id, n.ADMIN_STATE_UP AS admin_state_up, n.MTU AS mtu, sa.UPDATED_AT AS standard_attr_updated_at, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY n.TENANT_ID ORDER BY n.NAME) AS window_column FROM NETWORKS AS n JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = n.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE n.STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND n.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')), AdminUpTenantPorts AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT n.network_id, n.network_name, n.tenant_id, n.admin_state_up AS network_admin_state_up, n.mtu, n.standard_attr_updated_at AS network_standard_attr_updated_at, n.window_column AS network_row_number_within_tenant, p.PORT_ID AS port_id, p.HOST AS port_host, p.VIF_TYPE AS port_vif_type, p.VNIC_TYPE AS port_vnic_type, p.MAC_ADDRESS AS port_mac_address, p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS port_device_owner, p.window_column AS port_avg_standard_attr_id_window FROM ActiveTenantNetworks AS n JOIN AdminUpTenantPorts AS p ON p.NETWORK_ID = n.network_id AND p.TENANT_ID = n.tenant_id ORDER BY n.network_name, p.PORT_ID
WITH subnet_details AS (SELECT s.NAME, s.TENANT_ID, s.NETWORK_ID, s.IP_VERSION, s.CIDR, s.GATEWAY_IP, s.ENABLE_DHCP, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, MAX(s.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (ORDER BY s.ID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS max_standard_attr_id_next2 FROM SUBNETS AS s JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')), subnet_routes AS (SELECT r.DESTINATION, r.NEXTHOP, s.NAME AS subnet_name, s.CIDR AS subnet_cidr, p.FIRST_IP, p.LAST_IP, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY r.SUBNET_ID ORDER BY r.SUBNET_ID) AS window_column FROM SUBNETROUTES AS r JOIN SUBNETS AS s ON r.SUBNET_ID = s.ID JOIN IPALLOCATIONPOOLS AS p ON s.ID = p.SUBNET_ID WHERE s.IP_VERSION = 4 AND s.ID IN ('012993ae-5d73-4aad-834b-e87a31e99d48')) SELECT sd.NAME, sd.TENANT_ID, sd.NETWORK_ID, sd.IP_VERSION, sd.CIDR, sd.GATEWAY_IP, sd.ENABLE_DHCP, sd.FIRST_IP AS allocation_first_ip, sd.LAST_IP AS allocation_last_ip, sd.max_standard_attr_id_next2, sr.DESTINATION AS route_destination, sr.NEXTHOP AS route_nexthop, sr.window_column AS route_dense_rank_within_subnet FROM subnet_details AS sd JOIN subnet_routes AS sr ON sd.NAME = sr.subnet_name AND sd.CIDR = sr.subnet_cidr AND sd.FIRST_IP = sr.FIRST_IP AND sd.LAST_IP = sr.LAST_IP
WITH PortSecurityAndDns AS (SELECT p.id AS port_id, p.tenant_id, p.name AS port_name, COUNT(sgpb.security_group_id) AS security_group_binding_count, SUM(CASE WHEN pd.port_id IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS dns_record_count FROM SECURITYGROUPPORTBINDINGS AS sgpb JOIN PORTS AS p ON sgpb.port_id = p.id LEFT JOIN PORTDNSES AS pd ON p.id = pd.port_id WHERE p.admin_state_up = 1 AND p.tenant_id IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57') GROUP BY p.id, p.tenant_id, p.name), PortBindingWindow AS (SELECT b.PORT_ID, b.HOST, b.VIF_TYPE, b.VNIC_TYPE, p.TENANT_ID, p.NETWORK_ID, p.MAC_ADDRESS, p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER, AVG(p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID) OVER (PARTITION BY b.VIF_TYPE ORDER BY p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING) AS window_column FROM ML2_PORT_BINDINGS AS b JOIN PORTS AS p ON b.PORT_ID = p.ID JOIN PORTSECURITYBINDINGS AS psb ON p.ID = psb.PORT_ID WHERE p.ADMIN_STATE_UP = 1 AND p.TENANT_ID IN ('98333a1a28e746fa8c629c83a818ad57')) SELECT pbw.PORT_ID, pbw.TENANT_ID, psd.port_name, pbw.HOST, pbw.VIF_TYPE, pbw.VNIC_TYPE, pbw.NETWORK_ID, pbw.MAC_ADDRESS, pbw.STATUS, pbw.DEVICE_OWNER, pbw.window_column, psd.security_group_binding_count, psd.dns_record_count FROM PortBindingWindow AS pbw JOIN PortSecurityAndDns AS psd ON pbw.PORT_ID = psd.port_id AND pbw.TENANT_ID = psd.tenant_id
WITH PortStatusOwnerAgg AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER), ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS (SELECT n.name, n.status, n.admin_state_up, r.action, r.target_tenant, DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY n.id ORDER BY n.id) AS window_column FROM networksecuritybindings AS b JOIN networks AS n ON b.network_id = n.id JOIN networkrbacs AS r ON n.id = r.object_id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))), PortStatusOwnerAggForActiveNetworks AS (SELECT p.STATUS AS port_status, p.DEVICE_OWNER AS device_owner, COUNT(p.ID) AS port_count, MIN(sa.UPDATED_AT) AS earliest_standard_attr_update FROM PORTS AS p JOIN STANDARDATTRIBUTES AS sa ON sa.ID = p.STANDARD_ATTR_ID WHERE sa.RESOURCE_TYPE = 'ports' AND p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT DISTINCT r.object_id FROM networkrbacs AS r JOIN networks AS n ON n.id = r.object_id JOIN networksecuritybindings AS b ON b.network_id = n.id WHERE n.status = 'ACTIVE' AND NOT n.id IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b'))) AND NOT p.NETWORK_ID IN (SELECT networkrbacs.object_id FROM networkrbacs WHERE networkrbacs.action = 'access_as_shared' AND (networkrbacs.target_tenant = '*' OR networkrbacs.target_tenant = '9f13bf11e06e4d81b44c8c3e62d4362b')) GROUP BY p.STATUS, p.DEVICE_OWNER) SELECT d.name AS network_name, d.status AS network_status, d.admin_state_up, d.action AS rbac_action, d.target_tenant, d.window_column, a.port_status, a.device_owner, a.port_count, a.earliest_standard_attr_update FROM ActiveNetworkRbacDetails AS d CROSS JOIN PortStatusOwnerAggForActiveNetworks AS a ORDER BY d.name, d.action, d.target_tenant, a.port_status, a.device_owner
